# Eldmark: backlog

List of all pending and finished work, by phase and by area. In each session, items are picked from the current phase respecting their dependencies, the checkbox is ticked when done, and new items are added with the next free number of their prefix (existing ones are never renumbered). When a pending decision is closed, it moves to "Decisions made". Before deciding, check [vision.md](vision.md), [world.md](world.md), [networking.md](networking.md), [art.md](art.md) and, for anything about text and languages, [localization.md](localization.md).

Legend: priority essential, important or nice-to-have; size S (one session), M (1-2 sessions), L (2-3 sessions); layers shared, client, server, content, tools, operations; [x] done, [ ] pending and "(in progress)" after the title.

## Where we are

Eldmark has been public since 5 November 2026 (MIT code, project-made assets CC BY 4.0, everything in English). Phases 2 to 5 are in `main`: network, combat, the Vado Gris fort with its NPCs, abilities, chat and groups, and the platform service in Go ([history/2026-10-night-report.md](history/2026-10-night-report.md)). On 1 November 2026 the game was played for the first time with a server, 8 bots and a client on Windows, and the quality plan ([quality.md](quality.md)) came out of auditing those captures. Its waves 0 to 5 (animation, map, HUD, hit feel and scenery) are in `main`; wave 6, a visual polish pass over the HUD, war panels, map and terrain, is in `main` too. The 6 tests that failed on Windows were fixed on 3 November, and continuous integration on `main` is green: four Godot shards, the Windows `LocalServerLauncher` job, the Go platform and its end-to-end tests.

The English and Spanish localization (UI-04, plan in [localization.md](localization.md)) goes ahead in small increments that end with captures of the same screens in both languages. L1a (UI-57: the `L10n` runtime and the settings window) is in `main`; L1b and L1c (TEC-55, TEC-56) are in progress.

On 6 November 2026 the checkboxes of phases 2 to 5 were audited against their tests: what meets its criterion is closed with the tests that prove it, and what does not carries an exact "Missing:" (CMB-07 per-type resistances, UI-10, UI-13, UI-18, ART-58, TEC-19, PRG-09 and TEC-54). The batch in progress adds the repository's own continuous integration (TEC-66: Dependabot, CodeQL, govulncheck and a nightly Windows run) and works on the executables and container (TEC-12, TEC-20), the in-game menu (UI-06) and the split of `zone_server.gd` (TEC-65, which that lane adds to this file). Of the 19 items still open in the first-playable slice, the critical path to a closed test is the export and the test server (TEC-12, TEC-20), the Ford Captain (MUN-11) and the balance pass (CMB-29).

The ones with work done carry their "Missing:" on their own line.

Next batch, the fun one: abilities (CMB-19, CMB-21, ART-12, UI-11); kills and scoreboard (GUE-05, PRG-09, UI-41); GUE-41 and GUE-42; the Hollow (MUN-10) and settings (UI-16); chat (SOC-03, SOC-04, SOC-05); TEC-42, MUN-41 and CMB-41; bow (CMB-22, CMB-23, ART-41), knocked down (CMB-28) and pings (SOC-10).

## By area

### Interface (UI)

- [x] **UI-01 Exploration HUD and default actions** · F1 · essential · S · client — Crosshair that hides when the cursor is free, F3 debug panel (FPS, position, speed, ground, yaw), key help line and registration of default actions with physical keys (InputBindings, which never overrides an existing action). Already exists in scripts/client/hud.gd, crosshair.gd, input_bindings.gd and scenes/ui/hud.tscn.
- [ ] **UI-03 Interface manager and input contexts** · F2 · essential · M · client — UIRoot with layers (HUD, windows, modals, alerts, loading) and a window stack: opening a window releases the cursor through CameraRig and closing the last one recaptures it, Esc closes the top window before opening the menu, and while a text field has focus LocalPlayerController sends neutral input; it is mounted the same way in ClientMain and OnlineMain. Finished with tests of the mouse mode, the Esc order and the input lock. Depends on: UI-01, TEC-09.
- [ ] **UI-04 English and Spanish localization (umbrella; absorbs TEC-53)** · F5 · important · L · client, shared, server, content, tools — Plan in [localization.md](localization.md):
  - gettext with English as the source text (`msgid`) and `locale/es.po` holding today's Spanish texts verbatim;
  - the catalog lives in a private translation domain, so the engine never translates anything by itself and everything goes through `L10n`, which also does the pseudolocalization;
  - a "Language" setting that switches live; "Automatic" means Spanish until UI-39 and follows the system afterwards, with English as the fallback;
  - server messages travel as a code plus parameters that the client composes (protocol 8);
  - text fields of the .tres files translated by context, without duplicating resources;
  - tests always in English, a strict mode that fails when a lookup has no Spanish entry, a Spanish snapshot of every migrated screen and a pseudolocalization test.
  It is done in small increments that are merged and tested on their own, each with captures of the same screens in English and Spanish archived in Eldmark-capturas: UI-57 (L1a), TEC-55 (L1b), TEC-56 (L1c), UI-58 (L2a), UI-59 (L2b), UI-60 (L2c), UI-61 (L3a), UI-62 (L3b), TEC-57 (L4a), TEC-58 (L4b), TEC-59 (L4c), TEC-60 (L4d), UI-63 (L5a), UI-64 (L5b), UI-65 (L6a), UI-66 (L6b), TEC-61 (L7a), TEC-62 (L7b), TEC-63 (L8a) and TEC-64 (L8b), plus the final review UI-39. Finished when all of them are done and the scan for unmarked text covers all of `scripts/client`, `scenes` and `data`. Depends on: TEC-04, TEC-51, TEC-54.
- [ ] **UI-05 Connection screen and realm selection** · F2 · important · S · client — Polishes the RealmSelect from TEC-45: name validated with the SOC-01 rules and translated rejections. The realm cards with lore and emblem belong to UI-24. Finished when an invalid name is rejected with a clear text before connecting. Depends on: TEC-45, TEC-55, UI-60, SOC-01.
- [ ] **UI-06 Loading screens, disconnection and game menu** · F2 · essential · M · client — Loading screen with the zone's name and illustration, phase ("Conectando", "Cargando zona" with the progress from ResourceLoader.load_threaded_get_status, "Entrando") and tip; notice of a lost connection or a server shutting down with Retry/Back; game menu (Continue, Settings, Disconnect with CLIENT_LEAVE, Quit with confirmation) and a small ping indicator. Finished when connecting, disconnecting and killing the server always lead to a screen with an exit, never to a frozen image. Depends on: UI-03, TEC-09, TEC-45.
- [x] **UI-07 Player nameplates** · F2 · essential · M · client — Nameplates over remote players (never one's own) with name, emblem and realm color, anchored above the head at the interpolated position, fading with distance (allies up to about 40 m), hidden if a spaced ray detects occlusion, and applying whatever enemy-name rule is decided. Finished when, with 20 bots, the nameplates follow the remotes without jitter and disappear behind walls. Depends on: TEC-09, GUE-02. Done (K4): Nameplates/Nameplate in the HUD with a pool of 32 plates, the remote's interpolated position projected with the camera, allies up to 40 m with a bar, enemies with a name only under the crosshair and with a bar under the crosshair or when damaged in the last 5 s, occlusion with one ray every 6 frames and staggered; test_nameplates covers it with synthetic remotes (not with 20 real bots).
- [ ] **UI-08 System alert center** · F2 · important · S · client — NotificationCenter in the alert layer: toasts in a corner and a central banner for important events, with a priority queue, duration, merging of repeats and session history, connected to NOTICE (informational, kick, server shutting down). Finished with a test that queues alerts of different priorities and checks order and expiry. Quality (docs/quality.md): the `WarBanners` war banners go through the same queue (one at a time), over a dark band with an emblem and the title font, without the fade that today muddies letters and outline at once (`interfaz/hud_en_pelea_02.png`). Depends on: UI-03, TEC-57, TEC-09. Done (quality wave 3, the war banners part): `WarBanners` no longer stacks: a queue of 3 at most, one at a time, over a dark band with the side's emblem on both sides, the title font and a short fade of the whole band; time only runs for the one that is visible. On the compass, icons in a row separate from the letters, a numeric bearing under the needle, letters that fade at the extremes and markers merged at the same bearing. A compact, always visible objective line under the compass ("Capitán del Vado en pie · Puerta 72 %" (Ford Captain standing · Gate 72 %)) replaces the panel when far from the fort. Finished when in the fight captures no place label is within 150 px of the crosshair and there are tests of the filter and the merging. Depends on: UI-18, UI-19, UI-49. Done (quality wave 1): `WorldMarkers` filter (`pick_objective` and `select_shown`, with tests in `test_ui_quick_fixes`): by default only the icon of the current objective (the capture circle if active and, if not, the nearest fort that is not the player's own realm's) with its distance; the name only within 80 px of the crosshair or with Alt; the other places only appear, with a name, under the crosshair or with Alt and never stuck to the edge; at most 3 at once and no two icons within 240 x 24 px; for 5 s after a hit dealt or received (`notify_combat`, called by the `HUD`) only the objective and without names. Missing: the compass (icons in a separate row, numeric bearing, letters that fade and merged markers), the objective line under the compass, not marking the enemy camp or pass, the lower HUD margins and the merging of those that touch. **Done (wave 3):** compass in three rows (strip with letters that fade, a separate icon row with merging by bearing and a plate with the numeric bearing under the needle), `ObjectiveLine` under the compass when far from the fort ("Fuerte · De los Huecos · Capitán del Vado en pie · Puerta 72 % · 182 m" (Fort · Of the Hollow · Ford Captain standing · Gate 72 % · 182 m)), `WorldMarkers` without enemy camp or pass, with a 150 px bottom margin and with the label always 150 px or more from the crosshair (`push_from_aim`, with a guide line). The capture panel moves down to y = 68 so as not to overlap the new compass.

- [x] **UI-57 Localization L1a: translation base, language setting and settings window** · F5 · important · M · client, tools — First increment of UI-04 ([localization.md](localization.md) section 14):
  - `tests/test_l10n_runtime.gd` with what was checked in headless Godot 4.7.2: the .po loads without an import, the private domain translates with context and plurals without translating nodes by itself (in the main domain, orphan nodes are translated even with `root_node_auto_translate` off), `get_message` returns an empty string when the entry is missing, `OS.get_locale()` returns "es" and `set_locale` does not notify when the locale does not change;
  - `L10n` (`scripts/client/l10n/l10n.gd`): the `eldmark` domain, install on first use (galleries and previews too), `force` for `--locale` and the tests, `set_language`, translation with context, plurals and named placeholders in a single pass, `bind` that reapplies when the node returns to the tree, `raw`, own pseudolocalization (respects `{name}`, `%d` and BBCode, plurals too, Latin-1 accents only and `¦…¦` marks), strict mode and number formatting;
  - `locale/es.po` written by hand with the settings window (about 70 entries); the template arrives in TEC-55;
  - "Language" setting (Automatic, English, Español) in the Interface tab, which is saved, applied before the first screen and switches live from its own selector (deferred application and texts changed in place, without rebuilding the `OptionButton`). "Restore all" does not touch it. Until UI-39, "Automatic" is Spanish;
  - `--locale=` (en, es, pseudo) in `boot.gd`, which today warns "Argumento desconocido";
  - the settings window (F10) migrated by hand: the schema stores `msgid`, context and parameters instead of already formatted text ("Volumen: %s" becomes one label per bus of `audio_buses.gd`, and "%d FPS" is composed when shown), tabs with an id and `set_tab_title`, and `UIConfirmDialog` translates its default texts when it opens;
  - tests in English whatever the machine: `run_tests.gd` forces English, turns strict mode on and uses a temporary `settings.cfg` (`ClientSettings.default_path`), and the client processes the tests launch carry `--locale=en`;
  - `tests/test_l10n_screens.gd` with the Spanish snapshot of the window (taken before migrating it) and the pseudolocalization test with the switch made from its selector; `tests/test_l10n_glyphs.gd` with the `UIFonts` fonts; new rules in `test_architecture.gd` (no `\btr\(` in `scripts/`, no `L10n` or `TranslationServer` in shared, world and server); export filters (`locale/*.po` in the client, `locale/*` out of the server);
  - bench: `run.py --locale`, settings captures named after the tab id (the same names the archive already has) and a light capture of F10 over the title screen in `sc_front.gd`;
  - `docs/localization.md` and the rule for interface text in `CLAUDE.md` and `CONTRIBUTING.md`.
  Finished when:
  - the settings window shows in full in English, Spanish and pseudolocalization, and its Spanish snapshot is identical to the one from before;
  - choosing English in its own selector changes the open window, is remembered after a restart and `--locale` forces it without saving it;
  - the suite passes in English with any `settings.cfg` and fails when a lookup has no Spanish entry;
  - an exported client loads `es.po`;
  - the four tabs are captured in Spanish, English and pseudo, side by side, and archived.
  Depends on: TEC-51, TEC-54.

- [ ] **UI-47 Interface windows do not suspend movement** · F4 · important · S · client — Finding from SOC-05: `UIWindow` (settings F10), the map and the dialogs only release the cursor with `CameraRig.set_ui_cursor_release`, but `LocalPlayerController` reads the movement keys directly, so with W held the character keeps walking while a window opens. `LocalPlayerController.set_input_blocked` (SOC-05, per requester) already suspends game input; what is missing is deciding which windows must request it (settings and dialogs yes; the map and the scoreboard, perhaps not) and doing it. Finished when opening the settings leaves the character still and a test checks it. Depends on: SOC-05, UI-09. Phase 4 review (client), a case already resolved: with the social menu open (its first button has focus) Space jumped and also pressed "Invitar al grupo" (Invite to the group); `InputBindings.ensure_defaults` removes Space from `ui_accept` (Enter still confirms), `test_social_menu`. Deciding which windows suspend movement is still pending.

### Combat and abilities (CMB)

- [x] **CMB-01 Combat inputs and aim point** · F1 · essential · S · shared, client — PlayerInput already carries the PRIMARY, BLOCK, DODGE, ABILITY_1-3 and INTERACT buttons (bits 2-8) and the camera pitch; InputBindings assigns them (left and right click, Ctrl, 1-3, E), and the HUD and CameraRig.get_aim_ray() give the aim point. It is finished: all that is missing is for the simulation to consume those buttons (CMB-03).
- [x] **CMB-02 Data-driven combat catalog** · F3 · essential · M · shared, content — Resources in scripts/shared/combat/ with .tres data in data/combat/: WeaponDefinition (type, hands, block mitigation, attacks and abilities), AttackDefinition (windup, active and recovery, combo window, hit shape, damage, stamina cost, impulse, poise) and AbilityDefinition (cost, cooldown, casting, channeling, range, area, effects). A CombatCatalog assigns stable u16 IDs and adds its hash to the sim_hash. Finished when the sword and shield exists in .tres and a headless test loads the catalog, validates ranges and detects duplicate IDs, with test_architecture green. Only the data that affects prediction enters the sim_hash (phases, movement, stamina costs, cooldowns); damage, mitigation and multipliers are server-only. Durations in seconds, converted to 60 Hz ticks with fixed rounding. Depends on: CMB-01.
- [x] **CMB-03 Action state machine in the shared simulation** · F3 · essential · L · shared — Deterministic component called by PlayerCharacter.simulate(). It stores the current action (none, attack, block, dodge, ability, control or dead), its id, the start tick and the phase, with an input buffer of about 8 ticks and cancel windows; actions limit or replace movement (speed while blocking, displacement by data curve, limited turning when striking). All of it enters PlayerState.encode() and bumps PROTOCOL_VERSION. Finished with replay tests (set_state with the same inputs gives the same encode) and reconciliation tests with an action half done. Depends on: CMB-02, TEC-07.
- [x] **CMB-04 Stamina and exhaustion** · F3 · essential · S · shared — Integer stamina in PlayerState (0-1000) with costs taken from the data (dodge, heavy attack, block according to the damage absorbed) and regeneration after a delay in ticks. On reaching 0 there is exhaustion: no dodge or block until a threshold is recovered and with slower regeneration, the same on client and server. Finished with consumption, regeneration and exhaustion tests and with the value visible in the F3 panel. Depends on: CMB-03. Done: the stamina value is also visible in the HUD's F3 panel (K4).
- [x] **CMB-05 Hit geometry and line of sight** · F3 · essential · M · shared — scripts/shared/combat/hit_shapes.gd, pure math without physics bodies: annular sector with height (the weapon's arc) swept between active ticks against a capsule, segment against capsule (projectiles), oriented box (structures) and line of sight by raycast against the world layer from the chest. Each target receives at most one hit per action, in deterministic order by net_id. Finished with unit tests of edge cases: angle edge, height, target behind a wall and a fast hit that does not skip over the target. Depends on: CMB-02. Closed (2026-11-06): `test_hit_shapes` (angle edge, height, reach with the capsule radius, inner gap, line of sight blocked by a real wall, segment and capsule entry fractions) and `test_hit_shapes::test_a_fast_segment_does_not_skip_over_a_thin_target` (a segment of up to 2 m per tick crosses the capsule with both ends clear of it), `test_hit_shapes_box` (structures), and one hit per action and target in `test_combat_resolver::test_one_impact_per_action_and_target`.
- [x] **CMB-06 Light combo attacks and charged heavy attack** · F3 · essential · M · shared, content — For sword and shield: a chain of 3 light hits with a link window and a heavy hit when holding PRIMARY (minimum and maximum charge in ticks; damage scales with the charge). Includes a short advance, limited turning and cancel to dodge only in the recovery, all read from the .tres files. Finished when a test with scripted inputs runs through the combo and the charge checking the exact tick of each phase, and the sandbox demo shows it. Depends on: CMB-03, CMB-04, CMB-05. Done: the sandbox demo (DemoInput and RouteDemoInput) includes the 3-light combo, a charged heavy, a block and a dodge (CombatDemoScript, K4); the test_world one goes from 630 to 803 ticks. K5: `--demo --quit-after-demo` without a window, in test_world (803 ticks, about 14 s) and in Vado Gris (2049 ticks, about 36 s), ends by itself with code 0 and no errors, and writes the actions the character went through (LIGHT x4, CHARGE, HEAVY, BLOCK, DODGE); a real process checks it in tests/test_combat_demo.gd.
- [ ] **CMB-07 Damage, mitigation and critical formulas (in progress)** · F3 · essential · S · shared, content — scripts/shared/combat/damage_formula.gd and CombatRules.tres: damage = attack base × charge × modifiers × (1 − block mitigation) × (1 − armor with diminishing returns). It has damage types (slashing, piercing, blunt, elemental) with their resistances, criticals according to the open decision and an integer result. Finished with a table of test cases whose expected values are computed by hand. Depends on: CMB-02. Missing (verified 2026-11-06): per-type resistances. `DamageFormula.DamageType` exists, but `compute()` takes no resistance and `CombatRules` has no table, so SLASH, PIERCE, BLUNT and ELEMENTAL all behave the same (docs/combat.md section 4.4 keeps them at 0 in phase 3). The formula, armor, block, critical and minimum-1 cases are tested by hand in `test_damage_formula`.
- [x] **CMB-09 Authoritative resolution with hitbox rewind** · F3 · essential · L · server, shared — CombatResolver in scripts/server, in the tick's action step (networking.md §8.1). On each active tick it computes view_tick with view_offset_q and the cap from networking.md §10 and samples StateHistory, extended with the action state for block and invulnerability; then it tests the shape and the line of sight against the current world and emits the hits without moving bodies. Finished when an integration test with NetSim rtt=150 hits at least 95 % of the hits the attacker saw inside the arc against a target moving laterally, and rejects those exceeding 24 ticks. Depends on: CMB-05, CMB-06, TEC-06, TEC-08, TEC-10, TEC-13. Done: tests/test_combat_loopback.gd (real ENet, server NetSim with rtt=150 and jitter=30): 20 of 20 seen hits confirmed (8 of them would have missed without rewinding), mean rewind of 21.7 ticks, and 0 hits with a view_offset_q of 30 ticks (rejected by the cap with suspicion). The cap now uses the maximum of the RTT window in addition to the minimum (without that, with jitter 20 % of an honest bot's active ticks were rejected); with 200 ms of RTT the 24-tick cap no longer covers an honest player (networking.md, section 10). Phase 4 review ("cheats" lens): what goes over the cap up to `allowed + 8` is clamped to the cap instead of being rejected (an honest player with a hitch or low FPS chains up to 8 physics steps per frame and declared an older view: lost the hit or the arrow and added suspicion; it was the intermittent failure of the "rate" cheater in test_net_processes); rejection with suspicion starts at `allowed + 9`, so the cap test in test_combat_loopback now fakes 40 ticks instead of 30 (test_combat_resolver, test_projectiles).
- [x] **CMB-10 Health, networked combat events and in-combat state** · F3 · essential · M · server, client, shared — Authoritative health on the server, not predicted, in the snapshot's extra block (bit 3 of flags). Reliable ENTITY_EVENT 0x23 carries HIT (attacker, target, amount, type, critical, blocked) and DEATH, and on remotes it is applied at their render_tick; the record's action byte carries the state of remotes. The "in combat" state lasts 10 s after dealing or receiving damage, slows regeneration and blocks crossing zone passes. Finished with a loopback test in which attacker, victim and a third party receive the same HIT and see the same health. Adds out-of-combat health regeneration and exposes the "in combat" state (used by GUE-41, MUN-41 and TEC-30). Includes the "no damage is applied in a safe zone" test with GUE-03. Depends on: CMB-07, CMB-09, GUE-03, TEC-13. Closed (2026-11-06): `test_combat_loopback::test_a_bot_hits_another_and_attacker_victim_and_a_third_get_the_same_event` (the same HIT for the three and the victim's health in its extra block and in the third party's ledger), `test_combatant::test_health_regenerates_only_out_of_combat`, `test_combat_events` and `test_combat_director` (the in-combat state, 10 s), and `test_combat_server::test_a_hit_inside_the_safe_area_of_the_victims_realm_does_no_damage`. Blocking the crossing of zone passes while in combat belongs to the handoff, TEC-30.
- [x] **CMB-11 Dummies, combat log and test commands** · F3 · essential · M · server, client, tools — Includes a training dummy in test_world (fixed NPC NetEntity, with resettable health and an option to block or turn) and a combat log on the server (--combat-log=<csv>: tick, attacker, target, action, view_tick, rewind, damage). Adds a DPS meter in F3 and commands with --debug-commands: heal, kill, infinite stamina, reset cooldowns and change weapon. Finished when the DPS measured of the sword combo over 30 s against the dummy matches the one computed from the .tres files. Without the dummy (MUN-02) or the command parser (TEC-19): CSV log, DPS meter and combat commands registered in TEC-19. Depends on: CMB-10, MUN-02, TEC-19. Closed (2026-11-06): `test_combat_server::test_the_sword_combo_dps_against_the_dummy_matches_the_data` (and the one that detects a swing one tick longer), `test_combat_server::test_combat_log_and_report` (the CSV log), `test_combat_server::test_console_commands` and `test_cooldowns_off_and_apply_status_commands` (heal, kill, infinite stamina, cooldowns, weapon), `test_hud_combat` (the DPS meter over 30 s) and `test_combat_balance`.
- [x] **CMB-12 Control effects: stagger, stun, knockback and poise** · F3 · essential · M · shared, server — Stagger (interrupts the action), stun, knockback (imposed velocity) and poise (the heavy attack and certain abilities are not interrupted in their active phase). The server imposes them on its tick and they are stored in PlayerState so that the victim's client adopts them when reconciling; after chaining controls there is a brief immunity. Finished with tests: stagger cuts a light hit but not a heavy one with poise, and after two controls in a row the third does not enter. Depends on: CMB-03, CMB-09.
- [x] **CMB-13 Block, guard break and parry** · F3 · essential · M · shared, server, content — Block with BLOCK held: frontal angle and mitigation from WeaponDefinition (the shield stops 100 % of frontal physical damage; a weapon without a shield, part of it), reduced movement and a stamina cost proportional to the damage absorbed. When stamina runs out the guard breaks (short stun). The parry follows the open decision and the defensive check, the chosen rewind rule. Finished with tests: the frontal hit is blocked and the one from behind is not, the guard breaks with stamina at 0 and the parry works within its window and not outside it. Depends on: CMB-04, CMB-09, CMB-12.
- [x] **CMB-14 Dodge with invulnerability frames** · F3 · essential · M · shared, server — DODGE with direction according to WASD relative to the camera (backward without input) and displacement by data curve without root motion, using move_and_slide. The invulnerability ticks are recorded in StateHistory; the recovery can be cancelled with an attack, there is a stamina cost and it is prepared so that equipment weight changes distance and duration (CMB-31). Finished with tests: a hit on the invulnerable ticks does no damage and the dodge does not go through walls or the walkway blocks. Depends on: CMB-03, CMB-04, CMB-09.
- [x] **CMB-15 Death and respawn** · F3 · essential · M · server, client, shared — At 0 health the character switches to the dead action (ignores inputs) and DEATH is emitted with its author. After a configurable timer it respawns at a point of its realm with full health and stamina through the teleport counter, with a 3 s protection that is lost on attacking. Finished when a test kills a bot and it respawns at its camp after N ticks without taking damage during the protection. Includes the RespawnPoint nodes with realm and condition and the timer in ticks. A single 3 s protection that is lost on attacking; waves are added by GUE-14. Depends on: CMB-10, TEC-08, MUN-01. Closed (2026-11-06): `test_combat_director` (the requested respawn waits for its tick and uses the chosen point, the automatic one comes out at the first point), `test_combatant::test_death_and_respawn_bookkeeping`, `test_combat_resolver::test_dead_and_protected_targets_are_not_candidates` (the protection) and `test_fortress_scene::test_respawn_points_keep_the_camps_first_and_then_the_fort_interior` (the RespawnPoint nodes with realm and condition).
- [x] **CMB-41 Fall damage and lethal volumes** · F3 · important · S · shared, server — The shared simulation exposes the vertical speed on landing and the server applies damage above a threshold: the 3 m walkway does no damage and the 12 m tower does. Lethal volumes and falling below y = -100 kill instead of merely teleporting, and the death is credited to the last enemy that hit. Finished with tests of the threshold, of a credited fall death and of ramps and walkway doing no damage. Depends on: CMB-10.
- [x] **CMB-19 Ability system: slots, cooldown, casting and channeling** · F4 · essential · L · shared, server, content — Execution of AbilityDefinition in the action machine, with 3 slots (ABILITY_1-3) tied to the active weapon and cooldowns in ticks inside PlayerState, predicted and validated on the server. Effects are composed: hit with its own shape, charge, frontal or centered area, projectile, self effect, cast with a time and channeling that is cut by moving or by a control. Finished with tests: the cooldown survives a reconciliation, stagger cuts a channel and the server rejects an ability cast too early. Includes the cheating bot mode for cooldown (--bot-cheat=reuse: mashes an ability on cooldown) that does no extra damage, the one TEC-14 left for this phase. Depends on: CMB-03, CMB-09, CMB-12. Done (H1a): data and state without new behavior: `AbilityDefinition`/`AbilityTicks` (weapon and slot or consumable, synthesized hit with the ability's source, baked advance), `CombatState` v5 (ABILITY and SWAP actions, `ability_ready` of the two weapons in absolute ticks, 4-bit type buffer) in a 111 B `PlayerState`, `CombatCatalog` with ids by name, the validation of docs/abilities.md §2.2 and the hash with everything predicted, and `CombatSource`; test_combat_state_v5 and test_combat_catalog. Done (H1b): the predicted ability machine (`ActionMachine` split into `AbilityRules`): edge and buffer per slot, cooldown set at the commit (for channeling, at the end), cast, channeling that is released, breaks on moving if the data says so and is cut by a control setting its cooldown, the priorities of docs/abilities.md §3.3 and queries for the server and the bar (`active_hit`, `is_commit_tick`, `ability_status`); test_ability_machine and test_ability_reconciliation (the cooldown survives a reconciliation; stagger cuts a channel). The server rejecting and resolving it (H3a) and the cheating mode (H5) are missing. Done (H2): protocol 6 with the source of each hit (`CombatSource`: attack, ability, effect, finisher or bleed) in the `u16` of HIT, DEATH, KILL and TELEGRAPH and command 13 `cooldowns_off` (id and validation; the handler belongs to H3a); test_net_messages_v6. Done (H3a): the server resolves abilities: the synthesized hit (`AbilityTicks.hit`) that `Combatant.freeze` freezes goes through the resolver as an attack (rewind, defenses, gates), stuns or staggers, removes respawn protection and an interruption cuts the marked casts; self effects at the commit (step 3 of docs/abilities.md §4.1); command 13 `cooldowns_off`; test_ability_server (the server rejects an ability on cooldown: a bot that presses it one tick out of two commits exactly every `cooldown + cast` ticks). Of the "Finished when" the `reuse` cheating mode is missing (H5). Done (H3b): projectile abilities fire at their commit (a fan of three arrows at ±7°, a slowing arrow with its on-hit effect, a backward jump with a shot) through `ProjectileSystem`, with the ability's source in the SPAWN and in the HIT; test_projectiles. Done (H4): the action bar with the predicted cooldowns that a rejection reverts (test_action_bar), the provisional animations of each ability (`CombatClipPlanner`, one entry per name) and the animated remote with the slot from the action byte. Done (H5): the `--bot-cheat=reuse` cheating mode (mashes the shield strike one tick out of two next to the dummy): with ENet one hit comes out per cooldown, separated by exactly 738 ticks (`cooldown + cast`), with no extra damage, suspicion or prediction failures (test_bot_cheat). Combat bots use abilities by their role in the data and only when they are worth it (`BotBrain`, test_bot_brain_kit), also with bad network and without unexplained corrections (test_abilities_reconciliation). Done (H5 review, docs/abilities.md §11.10): after interrupting a cast, the slot (or the shared cooldown) waits `AbilityTicks.REARM_TICKS` = 6 ticks and the validation requires cooldown ≥ duration + 6, so that a remote always sees the NONE between two uses with the same action byte (test_action_wire, test_combat_catalog); test_abilities_reconciliation counts what the server casts and not what the bot meant to press. Phase 4 review (abilities): a tap of 3 shorter than the wall's cast (which ends without committing) also leaves the `REARM_TICKS` margin in its slot; before, another press came out with the same action byte after a single tick of NONE (test_action_wire, test_ability_machine).
- [x] **CMB-20 Status effects with duration, buffs and debuffs** · F4 · important · M · shared, server — StatusEffectDefinition (.tres) with duration in ticks, pulses (bleed, burn, healing over time), modifiers (slow, root, more damage, less mitigation), stacking rules (refresh, stack up to N, strongest wins) and cleanse. Those that touch movement live in PlayerState and the rest on the server; ENTITY_EVENT replicates them to show icons. Finished with tests: the slow gives the same speed on client and server and a bleed does exactly N pulses. Depends on: CMB-07, CMB-12. Done (H1a): `StatusEffectDefinition`/`StatusTicks` (u8 ids, duration an exact multiple of the pulses, ROOT and SLOW validated), the two movement effect slots in `CombatState` and `slowed`, `potion_regen`, `bleed` and `rooted` in data/combat/. Done (H1b): the movement slots in the machine (`impose_move_effect`, expiry on the exact tick, the third replaces the one that ends earlier) and `speed_factor`/`rooted` in `simulate`; `DamageFormula` with `damage_scale` and `mitigation_penalty`; test_move_effects (the slow gives the same speed on client and server). `StatusSystem` and the N-pulse bleed are missing (H3a). Done (H2): the ENTITY_EVENT STATUS (7: author, applied or removed, effect, stacks and `until_tick`) and HEAL (8) and command 14 `apply_status` (id and validation); test_net_messages_v6. Whoever sends them (`StatusSystem`, H3a) is missing. Done (H3a): `InputQueue.get_applied` (the input simulated at the shot's tick, for aiming), `HitShapes.is_ray_clear` with `hit_from_inside` (from the closed gate's doorway one neither hits nor is hit) and `Combatant.frozen.wall` (the shield wall's box); test_ability_server and test_input_queue. `ProjectileSystem` (H3b) is missing. Done (H3b): `ProjectileSystem` (scripts/server/projectile_system.gd): shot at the commit with the canonical ray of the simulated input, the crosshair's convergence and the exit from the chest, rewind fixed per shot in whole ticks (rejected or from the safe zone: harmless arrow), ballistics quantized like the SPAWN, flight by segments against the world and the gates (the first from inside), the wall's box and the rewound capsules with `StateHistory.position_at` and the boxes swept by blocks, invulnerability, frontal block without parry, caps of 8 per shooter and 64 per zone (the oldest goes out, DESPAWN 5) and DESPAWN 5 of the arrows of whoever leaves; transient ids from `NetEntityRegistry` and SPAWN/DESPAWN to whoever sees the shooter; test_projectiles (no arrow goes through a wall in 40 shots, the shot right against a corner hits the corner, the flight against rewound positions hits where without rewinding it fails), test_state_history and test_net_entity_registry. Of the "Finished when" the hit at 30 m with rtt=150 over the network (H5) and the predicted arrow (H4) are missing. Done (H4): `ProjectileWorld` (arrow predicted on each `ClientPrediction.stepped` with the commit, replaced by the server's with a decaying offset, withdrawn without SPAWN at rtt + 30; remote ones at their `render_tick` and withdrawn at `despawn_tick + f`) and `ProjectileLaunch` (what a commit fires and with what ballistics, shared); test_projectile_world. H3-H4 merge: `ProjectileSystem.fire` fires with `ProjectileLaunch` (`for_attack`/`for_ability` and `launch_toward` with its rewound candidates), so the server's arrow and the predicted one come out bit-for-bit equal with the same input and the same target (test_projectiles). The hit at 30 m with rtt=150 over the network is missing (H5). Done (H5): test_projectile_loopback: with 150 ms of RTT and 30 ms of jitter, 20 draws at 30 m against a target running laterally, aimed like a bot with `solve_lead` and `look_for` (`BotBrain.lead_look`): all 20 confirmed; against the present position (without rewinding) 0 would have hit; the 20 predicted arrows are replaced by the server's. Done (H5 review, docs/abilities.md §11.10): the remote that sticks in its origin (DESPAWN 4 on the shot's tick from inside the closed gate) uses `hit_from_inside` in its first segment and no longer goes through the gate; its fraction checks hostility with the SPAWN's realm (without the shooter's view, its allies no longer stop it); and the predicted one is considered rejected with the last snapshot's tick and not with the input's RTT (test_projectile_world). Phase 4 review (abilities): the shield wall of someone protected or in their realm's safe zone does not stop arrows (they pass as through its capsule); before, whoever held it in their camp spent stamina, ended up in combat and, without stamina, suffered the guard break, damage and even the knocked-down state (test_projectiles).

Done (H5 review, docs/abilities.md §11.10): starting to finish removes the respawn protection (otherwise nobody could cut the finish of someone who had just respawned) and the finish is cut if the knocked-down player crawls into their camp or whoever finishes enters theirs (test_downed). Phase 4 review (abilities): `DownedNotice` keeps each interaction on the knocked-down player with its actor and only that actor's end or cut removes it; the finish is shown before the revive (before, the reviver's cut erased "¡Te rematan!" with the finish underway; test_hud_combat).

No changes in the .tres; proposals P4 (potions) and P5 (fan) not applied. The K5 items (gate, captain, parry) and the session with people are missing, now with the two templates. Quality (docs/quality.md), three matters that touch balance and await a decision: (1) the sword connects at 3.0 m between axes (`range_m` 2.6 of `sword_hit_shape.tres` plus 0.4 of capsule) and the drawn blade reaches about 1.6 m (proposal: a 1.1 m sword and trail up to 2.2 m, without touching the range); (2) ART-46 lowers `lunge_speed` so that the attack's advance stays at 0.3-0.5 m; (3) if, when trimming the clips (ART-07), some attack matches no segment between 0.7x and 1.5x, its ticks may have to change, starting with the heavy. Each change repeats the `tools/combat_balance.gd` measurement.

### Progression, items and economy (PRG)

- [ ] **PRG-01 Stat sheet and shared formulas** · F4 · important · M · shared — StatBlock in scripts/shared/progression/ with base and derived stats (maximum health and stamina, regeneration, armor, power per weapon family, dodge cost and speed modifier). Flat and percentage modifiers are applied in a fixed order, and the pure damage and mitigation functions have diminishing returns; CMB calls them when validating a hit. Considered finished when the table-of-cases tests pass and test_architecture confirms it depends neither on the client nor on the clock. Only StatBlock and derived stats: the damage and mitigation functions belong to CMB-07. Depends on: CMB-04, CMB-07.
- [x] **PRG-04 Character equipment: authoritative state and replication** · F4 · important · M · shared, server, client — EquipmentState with slots (two weapons, head, torso, hands, legs and feet). The server validates the intent to swap weapons: it cannot be done during an action and it has a swap time. On equipping, the StatBlock is recomputed and the owner receives, by reliable message and with the tick at which it takes effect, the derived sheet that prediction uses (stamina, speed and dodge). Remotes only receive the visual ids of each slot. Considered finished when an integration test confirms that swapping weapons causes no prediction corrections. The validation of the weapon swap belongs to CMB-24. In phase 4 (docs/abilities.md §0 and §9) only the two weapons, in CombatState, without a derived sheet or armor slots (they arrive with PRG-06); the test is test_loadout_net (0 corrections around each SWAP without loss). Depends on: PRG-01, CMB-03, TEC-08. Done (H1a): the two weapons and the active slot in `CombatState` (`weapon_id`, `alt_weapon_id`, bit 0 of `combat_flags2`). Done (H1b): the machine validates the weapon swap (never in the middle of a hit, a charge, a cast or a channel; 0.4 s) and `set_loadout` imposes the two weapons on spawn; test_weapon_swap. Done (H2): the LOADOUT event (template, weapons in slot order and charges, which go to 0 for whoever is not the owner) and bit 3 of the record's `flags` so that remotes know which weapon they carry. Done (H3a): the foreign SPAWN carries the weapon pair and the active slot, not the cooldowns or the charges; test_net_entity. Done (H3c): the server imposes the template's two weapons on spawn and on respawn and command 5 `set_weapon` is `set_loadout` with the weapon in slot 1 (keeps slot 2; not with a knocked-down player), with LOADOUT to the owner with their charges and to whoever has it as relevant with 0; test_loadout_server and test_consumables_server. test_loadout_net is missing (H5). Done (H4): a remote's view stores its weapon pair (SPAWN and LOADOUT) and bit 3 of the record gives the one it holds in its hand (accessories and clips of the weapon), and the own LOADOUT explains the correction. test_loadout_net is missing (H5). Done (H5): test_loadout_net: with 100 ms of RTT and no loss, swapping weapons six times causes no prediction correction.
- [ ] **PRG-05 Equipment visual appearance** · F4 · essential · M · client — Client only. EquipmentVisual translates the ids of each slot into pieces on HumanoidVisual, which already mounts part_scenes on a single skeleton and handles the hood and the hair. The weapon and the shield go on the hands' BoneAttachment3D, are sheathed out of combat and each instance carries its realm's tint. Changing a piece does not recreate the character and works the same for the local player and for remotes. What is already done (HumanoidVisual pieces) belongs to ART-01; here the weapons and the equipment enter. Depends on: PRG-04, ART-06.
- [ ] **PRG-06 Armor and load categories** · F4 · important · M · shared, content — 15 armor pieces (5 slots for each of light, medium and heavy) that add armor and weight. The weight of the equipment worn sets the load category, which changes the dodge distance and cost and the stamina regeneration within the shared simulation, so it is predicted. Considered finished when there are tests that each category gives the expected dodge displacement, the same on client and server. It does not enter phase 5 (docs/progression.md §0): equipment slots 2 to 6 exist and are reserved, and visible armors will carry their own visual id, never the object's net id. Depends on: PRG-04, CMB-04, CMB-14.

The player chooses on spawn or respawn, the server validates and equips, and nothing is saved until phase 5. Done (H1b): `ActionMachine.set_loadout` and `reset_for_respawn` with the template's weapons and charges. Done (H2): LOADOUT_SELECT (`0x07`, 2 B: the template of the next spawn, 0 = the default one) and the LOADOUT event in protocol 6; test_net_messages_v6. The server (H3c) and the selector (H4) are missing. Done (H3a): the foreign SPAWN shows the two weapons and the active slot (what each client will see of the other's equipment). The template server (H3c) and the selector (H4) are missing. Done (H3c): the template server: LOADOUT_SELECT (also before CLIENT_READY; a notice with a template that does not exist or cannot be chosen) stores the pending one in `ServerPeer.pending_template_id`, which is applied on registration (the SPAWN already goes out with its weapons) and on each respawn; without choosing, the squire; fakes with `--fake-loadout=<template>[:<percentage>]`; LOADOUT with the template and the weapons; test_loadout_server. The selector (H4) and the two-client test (test_loadout_net, H5) are missing. Done (H4): `TemplateSelector` on the death screen (sends the LOADOUT_SELECT) and in the realm selection, and `--loadout=<template>`: the chosen one goes out in a LOADOUT_SELECT right after the WELCOME arrives, before CLIENT_READY; each remote's weapon accessories are visible; test_hud_combat, test_net_client, test_realm_select and test_client_config. The two-client test (test_loadout_net, H5) is missing. Done (H5): test_loadout_net: Ana chooses the squire before CLIENT_READY (`--loadout`) and Bruno the marksman already in the world (valid on respawn); each sees the other's weapons (the pair and the active slot). H5 review (docs/abilities.md §11.10): the squire is the default template and the test passed even if the server ignored the choice; now Ana chooses the marksman before CLIENT_READY and spawns with it (server, prediction and what Bruno sees), and afterwards both choose in the world (Bruno the marksman, Ana the squire) and they apply on respawn. Done (phase 5, progression.md P2): `StarterKit.grant`, the starter kit of progression.md §2.5 (the template's weapons equipped, those of the other templates and the potions in the bag, bound); the first entry into the zone is P5b's. Done (phase 5, progression.md P5b): the first entry gives the kit and, from then on, whoever has an inventory (every player with a peer; without a platform, ephemeral) spawns and respawns with their equipment: the template chosen before CLIENT_READY is only their kit and LOADOUT_SELECT in the world no longer changes anything; fakes continue with their template. test_loadout_net is adapted: Bruno equips the bow from his bag instead of choosing the marksman on respawn (progression.md §19.12). Done (phase 5, progression.md P6): with an inventory (from the first INVENTORY_FULL) the death screen no longer shows the template selector: the respawn takes the weapons from the equipment; the realm selection keeps it for the ephemeral character's kit (progression.md §19.19).
- [x] **PRG-08 Combat consumables** · F4 · important · S · shared, server — Health and stamina potions. Drinking is an action that slows and that is interrupted by a received hit. The cooldown is shared and validated by the server, and charges per life are replenished at the camp (in phase 4 they come in the template, without inventory; from phase 5 they are the potions in the bag, docs/progression.md §4.4). Considered finished when the cooldown and interruption tests pass. Depends on: CMB-03, CMB-10, UI-11. Done (H1b): drinking in the machine (charges, shared cooldown, commit on finishing drinking, +400 stamina predicted, without charges it does not start, the interruption before the commit spends nothing) and `set_charges`; test_consumables_machine. The interruption by hit on the server and the refill are missing (H3a, H3c). Done (H2): `CONSUMABLE_1` and `CONSUMABLE_2` travel in `VALID_BUTTONS` (the server already simulates drinking), bit 5 of HIT (interrupted), the charges in LOADOUT and command 16 `refill` (id and validation). Done (H3a): the health potion puts its healing over time on finishing drinking (`self_status`, 6 pulses of 50) and a damaging hit before the commit interrupts drinking without spending a charge or cooldown (bit 5 of HIT); test_ability_server and test_status_effects. The refill at the camp and test_consumables_server are missing (H3c). Done (H3c): the refill at the camp (every 60 ticks, alive, out of combat and in the safe zone of its realm: `set_charges` with the charges of its template, without touching weapon, cooldowns or the action in progress, and LOADOUT to the owner) and command 16 `refill`; test_consumables_server (the 6-pulse healing, an interrupting hit spends no charge or cooldown, the refill with slot 2 active and the wall raised). With test_consumables_machine (H1b), the cooldown and interruption tests pass. Done (H4): the R (`consumable_1`) and T (`consumable_2`) keys and their slots on the bar with the charges, the shared cooldown and the strike-through without charges; the provisional drinking animation (`Consume`). Done (H5): bots drink the health one below half and the stamina one below 30 %, only with charges and outside the cooldown; the `potion` cheating mode (mashes R and T) drinks once per shared cooldown and never without a charge (test_bot_cheat; since the H5 review, docs/abilities.md §11.10, with a second cheater that starts with 1 and 0 charges and drinks once and no more: before, the 5 charges were not exhausted in the window and removing the charge check did not make the test fail). Done (phase 5, progression.md P2): the potions as objects of the pure inventory: CAMP_REFILL restores the total of each (3 health and 2 stamina, splitting the stack gives no more) and `InventoryOps.remove_items` spends from the stack with the lowest slot (CONSUME); in the zone, with the charges `min(15, bag)`, in P5b. Done (phase 5, progression.md P5b): in the zone, for whoever has an inventory (every player with a peer), the charges are `min(15, bag)` of each potion, drinking spends one from the bag on commit (CONSUME; interrupted, nothing) and the camp refill is CAMP_REFILL from the bag at the same cadence; command 16 without a platform is a forced CAMP_REFILL (progression.md §19.12).

What belongs to PRG-09 itself is missing: the distribution by damage of the last 15 s, the anti-farming and their tests. - [ ] **PRG-19 Talent trees per weapon** · F6 · essential · L · shared, server, content — Tree as data: two branches per weapon family with active nodes (unlock abilities implemented by CMB) and passive ones (StatBlock modifiers), requirements per row and a final node in each branch. The player chooses three active abilities per weapon and can reset the tree by paying gold; the server validates everything. Considered finished when the trees of the two starting weapons are complete. Depends on: PRG-18, CMB-19.
- [ ] **PRG-20 Rarity, affixes and item level** · F6 · essential · L · shared, server, content — Five rarities, from common to legendary, that set how many affixes an object carries. Affix pools per slot and level range (attributes, armor, % damage of a family, dodge cost...). The server generates the object with a seed that is stored in the instance, and computes its item level and gear score. Considered finished when the reproducibility and distribution tests pass (10,000 rolls within the margin). Depends on: PRG-02, PRG-17.
- [ ] **PRG-21 Loot tables and delivery** · F6 · essential · M · shared, server, content — LootTable .tres with weighted entries, quantity, level and zone conditions, guaranteed drops and subtables. The server rolls when the NPC dies and gives personal loot to each player with a minimum contribution, in a bag in the world that is picked up with interact and expires. Considered finished when the seeded-roll tests pass and the one that another player cannot open your bag. Depends on: PRG-11, PRG-20, GUE-05, MUN-09.
- [ ] **PRG-22 Experience sources and PvE curve** · F6 · important · M · content, server, tools — XP per NPC according to level difference, per quest, per discovering places, per gathering and per crafting. The target hours per level is documented in docs/progression.md, and a script simulates the curve with the rates of the first realm's PvE zone. Considered finished when the script gives the expected time to reach that zone's cap. Depends on: PRG-13, PRG-21, MUN-17, MUN-25.
- [ ] **PRG-23 NPC merchants** · F6 · essential · M · server, content — VendorDefinition with currency, items, prices and limited stock that replenishes. Buying and selling requires being near the NPC and is transactional; what was sold during the session can be bought back. The selling price is a fraction of the base value, so that there are no gold loops. Considered finished when the capital's basic shops work (armorer, provisions and tools). Depends on: PRG-11, PRG-12, MUN-16.
- [ ] **PRG-24 Durability and repair** · F6 · important · S · shared, server — Durability lives in the instance: it drops a percentage on death and little by little with use, and a broken object contributes no stats. It is repaired at the armorer by paying gold (the main sink) or with craftable kits. Considered finished when there are tests of the loss and of the repair cost by rarity and level. Depends on: PRG-11, PRG-23.
- [ ] **PRG-25 Realm bank** · F6 · important · S · server — Storage per character, accessible at the bankers of the realm's cities and never in Eldrun. Transfers with the bag are transactional and more slots are bought with gold. Considered finished when a test deposits and withdraws from different capital channels and sees the same content. Depends on: PRG-11, MUN-19.
- [ ] **PRG-26 Gathering** · F6 · important · M · shared, server, content — Resource nodes (veins, choppable trees and herbs) as server entities: their available or depleted state is replicated through OBJECT_STATE and they respawn. Gathering requires the equipped tool (pickaxe, axe or sickle), is a channel that damage interrupts and gives gathering skill XP. Considered finished when three resource types are placed in the PvE zone. Includes placement by kit rules (MUN-45) and uses the generic OBJECT_STATE of GUE-09. Depends on: PRG-11, MUN-16, MUN-22, MUN-45, GUE-09.
- [ ] **PRG-27 Crafting, refining and professions** · F6 · important · M · shared, server, content — Recipes as data (inputs, output, station, profession level and time) and stations in the capital (smelter, forge, tannery, carpentry and alchemy). Raw materials are refined and equipment, consumables and repair kits are crafted. Professions have level and XP, and the level decides the quality or affixes of the result. Crafting is a transactional operation on the server. Considered finished when a raw material can be refined and used to craft a weapon, a consumable and a repair kit in the capital. Only refining, recipes, stations and transactional crafting; levels, XP and quality move to PRG-43. Depends on: PRG-20, PRG-26, MUN-21.
- [ ] **PRG-28 Trading between players** · F6 · important · M · server, shared — Trade window between two players of the same realm, within 5 m and out of combat. Double confirmation is needed, which is voided if the offer changes. The trade is atomic and is recorded in the ledger; bound objects cannot be offered. Considered finished when the tests of disconnection mid-trade and of an offer changed after confirming pass. Depends on: PRG-11, PRG-12.
- [ ] **PRG-29 Economy data validator** · F6 · important · M · tools, content — Automatic test that walks through objects, loot, recipes and shops: unique ids, valid references, coherent levels and no buy, craft and sell cycle that creates gold. If the recommendation of the open question is adopted, it includes a generator in tools/ that creates the .tres files from CSV tables to balance in a spreadsheet. Considered finished when the test passes with all of the first realm's content and detects a gold cycle seeded on purpose. Depends on: PRG-21, PRG-23, PRG-27.
- [ ] **PRG-30 Economic telemetry** · F6 · important · M · operations, server — Daily aggregates of the ledger by source and sink: gold created and destroyed, money supply, objects by rarity and trade prices. Anomaly alerts, such as a balance growing too fast or a repeated uid, and a report readable by the team. Considered finished when a day-of-bots report matches the sum of the ledger and the alert for a repeated uid seeded on purpose fires. Depends on: PRG-12, TEC-18.
- [ ] **PRG-43 Professions: level, XP and result quality** · F6 · important · M · server, shared — Comes from splitting PRG-27: level and XP of each profession, recipes that unlock by level and quality or affixes of the result according to that level. Finished when raising a profession unlocks a recipe and changes the quality of the result reproducibly. Depends on: PRG-27.
- [ ] **PRG-31 Power normalization in Eldrun** · F7 · essential · M · shared, server — The server applies the rule of the open question on entering Eldrun: it scales the level to the cap and limits the gear score, so that a veteran's advantage is bounded (target: at most 15 % more efficacy). Considered finished when the tests confirm that the same scaled sheet gives the same values on client and server. Depends on: PRG-13, PRG-20, GUE-20.
- [ ] **PRG-32 Realm ranks** · F7 · important · M · shared, server, content — Realm points that accumulate with merits and are not spent. Ranks give a title, a badge on the tabard and access to quartermaster tiers; their bonuses are horizontal only, so as not to break normalization. They are persisted and seen by allies and enemies. Considered finished when earning merits raises rank, the rank survives reconnecting and the badge is seen on another client. Depends on: PRG-09, PRG-10.
- [ ] **PRG-33 War quartermaster** · F7 · important · M · server, content — Shops at the wall gates and in own fortresses that sell for merits: bound war equipment with a normalized item level, consumables, repair kits and cosmetics with the realm's heraldry. There are items reserved for each rank. Considered finished when a player buys with merits in an own fortress and does not see the items of a rank above theirs. Depends on: PRG-23, PRG-31, PRG-32.
- [ ] **PRG-35 Fortress rewards and production** · F7 · important · M · server, content — Capture chest with personal loot according to contribution, castle lord loot, periodic merits for the defenders present and an extra for recovering isolated fortresses. Includes the siege materials of Odd's Quarry and the recipes for siege kits crafted by the realm; the weapon itself is implemented by GUE. Considered finished when capturing a test fortress distributes the chest by contribution and Odd's Quarry produces materials that serve to craft a siege kit. Depends on: PRG-21, PRG-27, GUE-22, GUE-23, GUE-25.
- [ ] **PRG-36 Realm market** · F8 · important · L · server, operations — Central service per realm with fixed-price sell orders, a fee on posting and on selling (sink), expiry with return to the bank, filtered search and delivery to the buyer's bank. Each operation goes in a database transaction, and it is accessed from the cities' auctioneers. Considered finished when a test buys the same lot at once from two processes and only one purchase goes through. Depends on: PRG-12, PRG-25, TEC-25, TEC-29, TEC-38.
- [ ] **PRG-37 End-game progression** · F8 · important · L · content, server — After the maximum level: gear score up to a cap, item upgrading with Embers and Eldrun materials, realm sets with a bonus by number of pieces, high-level crafting with a chosen affix and horizontal unlocks (titles and cosmetics). Considered finished when a max-level character can upgrade an object up to the score cap and complete a set, and in Eldrun normalization still bounds the advantage. When planning phase 8 it is split in three: gear score, cap and upgrading with Embers; realm sets and high-level crafting; horizontal unlocks. Depends on: PRG-20, PRG-27, PRG-35.
- [ ] **PRG-38 Vardheim items and loot** · F8 · important · L · content — Equipment with Vardheim's aesthetic per level range, loot tables for its PvE zones and dungeons, and the resources of its own biome. Finished when all that content passes the economy validator. Depends on: PRG-21, PRG-26, PRG-29, PRG-37, MUN-37, MUN-39, ART-27.
- [ ] **PRG-39 Economic anti-fraud** · F8 · important · M · server, operations — Detects and blocks duplicate uids, limits trade and the market for new characters, looks for patterns of gold selling for real money (repeated one-directional flows) and reverts transactions from the ledger while notifying a GM. Considered finished when a test seeds a duplicate uid and a suspicious flow and the system blocks, reverts and notifies. Depends on: PRG-28, PRG-30, PRG-36.
- [ ] **PRG-42 Achievements and titles** · F8 · important · M · server, client, content — Achievements as data (exploration, PvE, war, crafting) that the server evaluates with the same events as quests, titles visible on the nameplate and account unlocks, to give reasons to come back the next day. Finished when 30 achievements of zone 1-10 and Vado Gris are earned in a scripted match and the title is seen on another client. Depends on: MUN-17, PRG-10.
- [ ] **PRG-44 Solmarch items and loot** · F8 · important · L · content — Equipment with Solmarch's aesthetic per level range, loot tables for its PvE zones and dungeons, and the resources of its own biome. Finished when all that content passes the economy validator. Depends on: PRG-21, PRG-26, PRG-29, PRG-37, MUN-37, MUN-39, ART-27.
- [ ] **PRG-45 Askarra items and loot** · F8 · important · L · content — Equipment with Askarra's aesthetic per level range, loot tables for its PvE zones and dungeons, and the resources of its own biome. Finished when all that content passes the economy validator. Depends on: PRG-21, PRG-26, PRG-29, PRG-37, MUN-37, MUN-39, ART-27.
- [ ] **PRG-40 Appearance wardrobe and dyes** · F9 · nice-to-have · M · client, server, content — Allows wearing the appearance of any unlocked piece over the equipped one without changing stats, with craftable dyes. In Eldrun, the tabard and the side colors are always read above the chosen appearance, so as not to lose the readability of who is who. Considered finished when an applied appearance looks the same on another client without changing stats and in Eldrun the side tabard remains visible on top. Depends on: PRG-05, PRG-11.

### World, PvE and NPCs (MUN)

- [x] **MUN-01 Zone registry and spawn points per realm** · F2 · essential · S · shared, content — Shared `Zones` registry (scripts/shared/zones.gd) with default zone, scenes, realms and spawn points per realm (`spawn_points` group with `realm` metadata), plus `ZoneRoot`, which prepares Terrain3D on client and server. Done: the client spawns at its realm's camp in Vado Gris and test_zones and test_client_zone cover it.
- [x] **MUN-02 Networked NPC entity and training dummy** · F3 · essential · M · server, shared, client — NPC entity type on the server (NetEntity per stream; humanoids use the same record as players plus health and action byte), interpolated on the client with HumanoidVisual and recorded in StateHistory so that rewound hits reach it; first use, a training dummy at each realm's camp. Finished when two clients hit the dummy, both see its health drop and a single-process integration test validates a rewound hit against it. Depends on: TEC-06, TEC-08, TEC-09, CMB-09, CMB-10. Closed (2026-11-06): `test_combat_loopback::test_both_players_hit_the_dummy_and_every_snapshot_shows_its_health_drop` (real clients whose inputs carry their view tick hit the dummy and the server rejects none for exceeding the rewind cap), `test_combat_server::test_a_dummy_is_an_immortal_npc_simulated_with_the_players` (recorded in the StateHistory, so it is rewindable) and `test_combat_resolver::test_rewound_hit_lands_where_the_attacker_saw_the_target` (the rewind itself, between two fighters); the dummies stand at both camps of `vado_gris.tscn`.
- [ ] **MUN-04 Vado Gris: building and reviewing the zone (in progress)** · F4 · essential · L · content, tools — 300 × 300 m border zone generated by script (Terrain3D, horseshoe river, two bridges, ford, fort with FortGate and CaptureCircle, CaptainSpawn and GuardSpawn markers, walls, camps and ruins) with symmetric routes verified by verify_vado_gris_routes.gd. The review remains to be closed: replace the prismatic tents and the blockout banners and approve the review camera captures. The final tents and banners are ART-14 and ART-15; for the vertical slice the current gameplay layer is enough. Quality (docs/quality.md): the 1 November captures do not approve the review; what is missing is in ART-54 to ART-63. ART-56 (relief) and ART-57 (fort) touch the gameplay layer: each change repeats `verify_vado_gris_routes.gd`, the navigation mesh baking and the map baking. Depends on: MUN-01.
- [ ] **MUN-05 Vado Gris: separate gameplay layer and visual layer** · F4 · important · M · content, tools, server — Split vado_gris.tscn into vado_gris_gameplay.tscn (terrain with collision, collisions, markers, fort and passes) and vado_gris_visual.tscn (meshes, grass, edge trees, water, environment and lights), generated by the same script; the server loads only the gameplay one. Finished when test_vado_gris_zone passes with both layers, the headless server creates no Terrain3D instancer instances and the verified routes do not change. Includes the measurement of server memory. Depends on: MUN-04.
- [x] **MUN-06 NPC navigation on the server** · F4 · essential · M · server, shared, tools — NavigationMesh baked from the gameplay layer (terrain, ramps, walkway, bridges and ford; deep water excluded) saved as .tres, with NavigationRegion3D working in headless, a link through the fort gate that is activated according to the FortGate state and a shared routes API for the AI. Finished with a headless test that obtains valid routes from each GuardSpawn to the walkway, the ford and the two bridges, and that finds no way out of the fort with the gate closed. Bakes the navigation from layer 1 of the current scene. Done in F0: `tools/bake_navmesh.gd` and `tests/test_navmesh.gd` (the "Finished with" test); the running map with the gate link and the `get_path` API (`NpcNavigation`) belongs to the F4 package (docs/fortress.md, 4.1). Depends on: MUN-04. Done (F4): `NpcNavigation` (`scripts/server/ai/npc_navigation.gd`): own `NavigationServer3D` map with synchronous iterations, the region of `<scene>_navmesh.tres` and a link through each gate's doorway that follows `FortGate` (layer of the owning realm, or of everyone with the gate broken); `get_path(from, to, realm)`, `closest_point` and `poll()`. `tests/test_npc_navigation.gd` (7 tests): routes with the gate closed and open per realm, the broken gate and the map ready. Docs: docs/fortress.md 4.1 and 8.3.
- [x] **MUN-07 NPC AI architecture** · F4 · essential · L · server, shared, client, tools — Framework in scripts/server/ai/: NpcDefinition (.tres with health, speeds, perception radii, leash and attacks), hierarchical state machine (idle, patrol, alert, combat, return) that thinks at a configurable rate and produces intents, perception by radius, cone and line-of-sight raycast, threat table and a leash that returns the NPC to its post regenerated, plus a debug overlay (state, target, radii and route) with --debug-commands. Finished when a headless test with a seeded RNG reproduces exactly the same state sequence against a bot player. Depends on: MUN-02, MUN-06. Done (F4): `NpcDefinition` and `NpcCatalog` (`scripts/shared/ai/`, five definitions in `data/npcs/`: `hollow`, `ford_captain`, `gate_guardian`, `realm_guard`, `realm_captain`), `HfsmBrain` (IDLE, PATROL, ALERT, COMBAT, RETURN; thinks every 6 ticks staggered by `net_id`; perception by radius, cone and ray; threat; leash with immune and regenerated return), `NpcWorld` and the `npc_debug` debug (command 12, with `--debug-commands`). `test_hfsm_brain` (17 tests) checks that the same seed against a bot gives the same state sequence. Measured cost: 50 to 85 µs per NPC and tick (docs/fortress.md 8.3). Phase 4 review ("cheats" lens): an NPC hit from beyond its loss radius (an archer) raises it up to the archer and chases (before, it stood still healing every 3 s and died without moving with enough archers), and each brain's state log is bounded (256-511 transitions; it grew without limit); test_hfsm_brain. Done (phase 5, progression.md P5c): `xp_reward` and `gold_reward` in `NpcDefinition`, with the values of progression.md §6.2 in `data/npcs/`.
- [x] **MUN-08 Readable NPC melee combat** · F4 · essential · M · server, shared — Humanoid NPCs use the same actions as the player (light, heavy, block, dodge) with configurable telegraphed windup computed to absorb RTT plus interpolation delay (e.g. ≥ 450 ms light and ≥ 700 ms heavy), and a token system that limits to 2 the NPCs attacking the same player at once while the rest circle around. Finished when, with --net-sim=rtt=200, a bot that blocks 250 ms after seeing the windup blocks all heavy hits and 4 guards against 1 player never attack more than 2 at once. Depends on: MUN-07, CMB-06, CMB-13, CMB-14. Done (F4): NPC weapons (`data/combat/npc_*.tres`: 4 weapons, 8 attacks) with `AttackDefinition.unblockable` (server only, outside the hash), windup 0.45 to 1.0 s, the TELEGRAPH warning for every NPC heavy and `AttackTokens` (2 tokens per target, expire at 1 s). Tests: 4 guards against 1 player never attack more than 2 at once, and a bot that blocks 550 ms after seeing the hit begin (RTT 200 ms plus 250 ms of reaction) blocks all the heavies of a Hollow and a guard. The criterion with `--net-sim=rtt=200` was emulated with that delay in the bot (not over a real network). The own animations belong to ART-16.
- [x] **MUN-09 NPC spawners and enemy respawn** · F4 · essential · S · server, content — NpcSpawner node in the gameplay layer (marker, NpcDefinition, maximum alive, respawn in ticks, alert group and signal activation condition, e.g. the fort's owner) that creates and removes NPC entities on the server; GUE reuses it for the owning realm's guards. Finished with a test that kills NPCs and checks the exact respawn in ticks and that deactivating the spawner removes the living ones. Depends on: MUN-07. Done (F4): `NpcSpawnerSystem` (`scripts/server/ai/npc_spawner_system.gd`): one post per `NpcSpawner`, activation by condition (ALWAYS, FORT_NEUTRAL, FORT_OWNER) and by fortress signals, respawn on the exact tick (death + `respawn_s`), removal of the living and of corpses (300 ticks) on deactivation, and `--no-npcs`. `test_npc_system` (exact respawn in ticks, and deactivating removes the living ones) checks it.
- [ ] **MUN-10 The Hollow: neutral fort garrison (in progress)** · F4 · essential · M · server, content — Four Hollow at the existing GuardSpawn (two on the gate esplanade and two patrolling the walkway), group alert at 25 m, leash limited to the plateau so that they cannot be dragged to the bridges and full respawn if the fort stays neutral with no players within 40 m for 2 min. Finished when 2-3 players clear the garrison in 60-90 s in a playtest and a test confirms that the leash prevents getting them off the hill. Depends on: MUN-08, MUN-09, ART-16, GUE-08. Done (F4): four Hollow at the `GuardSpawn` (two on the esplanade, two patrolling the walkway by the ramps), group alert at 25 m, a 32 m leash from their post that returns them to the hill even if a runner drags them toward the ford (test on the real zone) and full respawn after 7200 ticks without players within 40 m of the circle with the fort neutral. 3 combat bots clear them in about 46 s of play. Missing: playing it with people to set the 60-90 s of the criterion (bots are no fine measure) and ART-16 (the Hollow's own model and animations).
- [ ] **MUN-11 Ford Captain: first boss (in progress)** · F4 · essential · M · server, client, content — Boss with a wide blockable sweep, an unblockable hit marked on the ground that forces dodging, a shout that alerts the living Hollow and a wave of 2 Hollow at 50 % health; on dying it emits the event that opens the capture circle and respawns when the fort changes owner. Finished when 3-5 players defeat it in 60-120 s, the telegraph marks are seen on the client and the death event reaches the capture logic. Includes the telegraphed attacks; criterion: 3-5 players defeat it in 2-3 min. The shout and the reinforcements only activate if there are living Hollow. It only appears with the fort neutral; with an owner, the captain is the realm's (GUE-11). Depends on: MUN-08, MUN-09, ART-09, ART-16, GUE-08. Done (F4): Ford Captain (`ford_captain`, 30,000 health): blockable sweep, unblockable hit every 3 attacks with TELEGRAPH (sector, 3.5 m, 1.0 s of warning; blocking does not stop it and dodging does), shout that alerts the living Hollow at 60 m, wave of 2 Hollow at 50 % health (only if any remain), regeneration if the party dies, death event to `FortressController` and respawn with the change of owner. 4 bots healed on each tick defeat it in about 78 s. Missing: playing it with people to set the 60-120 s, ART-09 and ART-16 (today it is seen with the humanoid) and seeing the telegraph marks on the client end to end (F6). Phase 4 review (war): reinforcements are once per life (returning regenerated to its post no longer rearms the call: each failed assault added two more Hollow), the full respawn removes them and gives back the call, and there are never more than two living reinforcements per fortress. Tests in `test_npc_system` (two).
- [x] **MUN-12 Camp safe zone and gate guardians** · F4 · important · M · server, content — Safe zone volume at each camp (radius 24 m in Vado Gris) where players of the owning realm take no damage from enemies, and two high-damage, long-reach NPC Gate Guardians that attack intruders. Finished when a test shows that incoming damage is nullified inside, that an enemy inside dies in under 10 s and that outside combat is normal. Only the Gate Guardians; the safe zone volume belongs to GUE-03. Depends on: MUN-08, GUE-03. Done (F4): two Gate Guardians (`gate_guardian`, 260 damage, reach 3.6 m, `ignores_safe_area`) that only see and attack enemies inside their realm's safe zone; an intruder dies in 4 to 5 s (under 10 s) and allies and those outside are not attacked. Incoming damage inside the safe zone was already nullified (GUE-03). `test_npc_system` checks it.
- [ ] **MUN-41 /stuck command (in progress)** · F4 · important · S · server, client — Out of combat, a 10 s channel that damage interrupts and an authoritative teleport to the nearest valid spawn point of the realm, with a 5 min cooldown. The client command is missing.

- [ ] **MUN-21 First realm capital** · F6 · essential · L · content — Capital (Valcorin or Isenholm, see open questions) made with the zone kit: 1 × 1 km playable, a 300-500 m city with market square, craftsmen, barracks with a war board, realm landmark, inn and stables, a safe zone without combat, arrival points for each pass and markers for the service NPCs.
