- [ ] **TEC-66 Continuous integration of the public repository (in progress)** · F5 · important · M · operations — The protection that a public repo needs, in `.github/`: `dependabot.yml` (weekly updates of the actions, the Go modules of `platform/` and the platform's Docker base images), `workflows/codeql.yml` (CodeQL for Go and for the workflow files, plus a `govulncheck` job over `platform/`, on pushes to `main`, pull requests and weekly) and `workflows/nightly.yml` (the whole suite on `windows-latest` in two parts and `load_test.gd --brawl` on Linux, whose report is kept as an artifact to see the trend; there is no threshold yet). Every action is pinned by commit SHA with its version in a comment. Finished when: CodeQL and govulncheck are green without findings that are reachable, the nightly run passes once, Dependabot opens its first pull requests, every action of every workflow (`tests.yml` included) is pinned by SHA, the job names are in English before anyone marks them as required checks (with the `grep` of the Summary step and the summary of `tests/run_tests.gd` adapted), and the owner has the list of repository settings that only the owner can change (branch rule with the required checks and admin bypass, secret scanning with push protection, Dependabot alerts). Done (6 November 2026): the three files above, tested on a branch (govulncheck, the full suite and the nightly run were green). CodeQL reported two high alerts (`go/incorrect-integer-conversion`, `platform/internal/config/config.go`, the zone token parser: the range was checked but not visible to the analysis), fixed by parsing with `strconv.ParseInt(..., 10, 16)`; they must show as fixed on the next analysis of `main`. The first govulncheck run found 50 reachable advisories (the Go 1.24.0 standard library, `x/text` 0.31 and `pgx` 5.8), fixed by moving `platform/` to Go 1.26.10, pgx 5.12 and x/text 0.43, including the Dockerfile's builder image. Missing: bumping `govulncheck` (installed by version in `codeql.yml`, which Dependabot does not track) by hand when a new one is out, pinning `tests.yml` and renaming its jobs (it is edited by localization L1b, TEC-55) and the summary of `run_tests.gd`.

- [x] **TEC-25 Character persistence** · F5 · essential · L · server — The zone loads the character when validating the ticket and saves it with versioned writes and an idempotency key. It saves every 60 s if there were changes, on leaving or disconnecting, and instantly on critical events (trade, rare loot). A lease gives each character to a single zone and the write queue does not block the tick. Finished when a kill -9 loses 60 s at most and no race test duplicates objects. Save every 60 s, on leaving and on critical events; the test kills the process without duplicating anything. Decided in platform.md §7: idempotency only by `(lease_id, seq)` with a lease; `save_version` is a counter that does not intervene. The critical events are those of progression.md §9.2 (phase 5: kit, level-up, capture and GM commands; trade, mail and rare loot from phase 6). Depends on: TEC-24, PRG-10. Done (phase 5, P0): `character_leases` with `last_seq`, `last_save_hash` and `last_save_version`, and `id_blocks` with `uid_block_seq` (0003_zones). Done (phase 5, P3): the internal API's save (`PUT /internal/v1/characters/{id}/state` and `POST /internal/v1/saves`): strict envelope, lease, idempotency by `(lease_id, seq)` with the body's hash and the lease's `save_version`, `pvp` with `greatest`, the error rule of §7.3 (retry of 40001/40P01, 503, `invalid_save` also at COMMIT), release, the heartbeat that renews leases and the lost-instances job, tested with the progress fake and 100 concurrent saves (platform.md §19.4). `CharacterSaver` in the zone (P5), the real store (progression.md P7) and the kill -9 test (P10) are missing. Done (phase 5, P4): in the zone, `ZonePlatform` and `PlatformClient` (registration, heartbeat with the lease set, fencing at 10 s, `save` that sends the bytes untouched and retries them until a definitive response, release, id blocks, priority queues that do not put the heartbeat or the claim behind saves, a single `instance_closed`) and the removal without save in `VERIFYING` and `LOADING` (platform.md §19.8). Done (phase 5, implementation review): the heartbeat no longer renews an expired lease (and the TTL has to cover how long it takes for an instance to be considered lost), the startup grace does not resurrect leases that expired before the crash, a pvp total that does not fit in its column is `envelope` and class 22 is `invalid_save` S8 instead of a 500 that the zone retried endlessly (platform.md §19.7). Done (phase 5, P5): `CharacterSaver` in the zone (platform.md §19.9). It saves at each character's checkpoint in its phase and only with something dirty, and on criticals in the tick, with one write in flight per character. The final goes out when the body leaves (also after a combat exit) with `release_lease` and waits behind the one in flight. The whole envelope of §7.3 is serialized once and retries send the same bytes; entries go by `lease_id`, so a reused `net_id` or the same character coming back receive no foreign responses. It has the response table of §7.3 with removal without save (kick with the body gone at once) and the `revoked` with a final save. The orderly shutdown of the five triggers and the §6.1 shutdown (finals before the NOTICE) wait for the platform to answer. Tests: test_character_saver, test_zone_server_saves and test_zone_process_shutdown. Until progression.md P5a there are no registered sections: the zone releases without saving, as in P4. The sections (progression.md P5a and P6), the real store (progression.md P7) and the kill -9 test (P10) are missing. Done (phase 5, progression.md P5a): the `progress` section registered first, with the kit as critical on registration, the load before the WELCOME (a record that cannot be read is REJECT `UNAVAILABLE` and release) and removal in LOADING with `cancel_prepared`; finals now save (progression.md §19.11). `pvp` (P6), the real store (progression.md P7) and the kill -9 test (P10) are missing. Done (phase 5, progression.md P5b): registration uses what was loaded (equipment, active slot, health, return-to-world rule and timers) and the inventory over the network enters the saves; without a platform, ephemeral characters (progression.md §19.12). Done (phase 5, P6): the `pvp` section of `ScoreKeeper`, registered behind `progress`: the claim's totals plus the session's (captures and defending time included), with the final built before `forget` and the reconnection that brings them (platform.md §19.11). Done (phase 5, progression.md P7): the real store: `Apply` with S1-S8 in the save's transaction (the locked character's progress, ids of the instance's blocks, a repeated uid or `op_id` as S8), `Load` in the claim and `serve` with it in all environments; `TestDuplicateUid` saves at once two characters with the same new uid and with the same `op_id` without duplicating anything (progression.md §19.17). Done (phase 5, integration): real saves end to end: by hand, with the Go platform, PostgreSQL and a `test_world` zone, the bot with an account enters, receives the kit (STARTER operation), three GM operations from the console go out in a critical save that passes S1-S8, leaving releases the lease with its final save, and the next entry loads what was saved (platform.md §19.17). The kill -9 test is missing (P10). Done (phase 5, P10): the kill -9 test (`TestZoneCrash`, `platform/e2e`): with the Go platform, PostgreSQL and real Godot processes, a zone with `--save-interval=5` killed with `kill -9` loses less than 5 s + 1 s (the last `saved_at` comes from a checkpoint, 0.5 to 1.1 s before the kill), another instance replaces it as soon as the platform considers the old one lost and the character re-enters in it; by SQL nothing is duplicated or left half done (Σ `count_delta` = Σ `count` of the live objects, Σ `delta` = balance, a single kit). Also `TestEnterAndSave` (enter, save and leave) and the "Plataforma e2e" job in CI (platform.md §19.19). The criterion is met: a kill -9 loses only what is after the last checkpoint (60 s at most with the default interval) and no race test duplicates objects (the 100 concurrent saves of P3 and `TestDuplicateUid` of progression.md P7). progression.md P8 adds `TestProgressCrash`, with economy operations before the kill.
- [x] **TEC-27 Transport encryption and secret management** · F5 · essential · M · server, client, operations — DTLS on ENet (dtls_server_setup with a certificate pinned on the client) and valid HTTPS on the platform; the CPU and byte cost is measured and the 1200 B cap adjusted. The HMAC secret, the DB and the keys go in environment variables or Docker secrets, with documented rotation. Finished when, with 64 bots, DTLS does not raise the tick's p99 by more than 10 % compared with the same test without DTLS and the absolute figure is recorded in networking.md §14, a mandatory condition before any public test (provisional criterion, taken at night: the p99 < 8 ms one is not met even without DTLS, 15.9 ms; pending from the user, platform.md §0 and §10.2). Depends on: TEC-09, TEC-20. Done (phase 5, P0): the platform reads each secret from its variable or from `<NAME>_FILE` (Docker secrets) and does not repeat it in errors or logs; `.gitignore` with the patterns of platform.md §10.3 and `tools/check_secrets.sh` in CI. DTLS and HTTPS, in P9c and P9b. Done (phase 5, P3): in `staging` and `production` the platform does not register a zone without a DTLS certificate (400) and stores the certificate that `enter` gives the client (platform.md §19.4). Done (phase 5, P4): the zone reads its token and the ticket keys from `ELDMARK_ZONE_TOKEN` and `ELDMARK_TICKET_KEYS` or from their `_FILE`, never from the command line, and does not start with the test vector's key outside `test` or with development options in `staging` or `production` (platform.md §19.8). Done (phase 5, P9b): HTTPS with Caddy and Let's Encrypt in front of the public API (`platform/deploy/Caddyfile`, no access log and with `X-Real-IP` only from its fixed IP), the secrets as files mounted with Docker secrets (`ELDMARK_*_FILE`) and the CI secrets check tested with a `staging.env` in the index (platform.md §19.13). DTLS, in P9c. Done (phase 5, P8): the client side of the contract of §10.2: `NetTransport.connect_to(…, dtls_name, dtls)` with `dtls_client_setup`, and `NetClient` pins the certificate from `enter` (or the one from `--dtls-cert`/`--dtls-name` without a platform) and does not connect in clear if it cannot read it; not tested against a zone with DTLS (the server is P9c's) (platform.md §19.16). Done (phase 5, P9c): DTLS in the zone: `NetTransport.host(…, dtls)` below `GuardedEnetPeer`, `--dtls-cert --dtls-key` in the zone (with `ZoneDtls`: the CN read from the DER, which goes with the PEM in the registration, and the key checked against the certificate; it exits with 1 if something is not valid), `--dtls-cert --dtls-name` in `--bots` (`SwarmBot`) and the disconnection that the server always starts (if the client starts it, the server engine logs an error on each exit). Measured with `tools/load_test.gd --dtls-compare`: with 64 real bots, DTLS raises the tick's p99 by 5.9 % (from 26.9 to 28.5 ms, three passes of each), and the absolute figure is in networking.md §14 with the cost per packet (+2.2 µs on receiving, +5.1 µs on sending a snapshot), the 29 B extra per datagram (the 1200 B cap does not change) and the handshake (2 ms of zone CPU with EC P-256, 4 ms with RSA 2048). It meets the provisional criterion; it remains for the user to confirm it (the absolute one is not met even without DTLS) (platform.md §19.18).
- [ ] **TEC-28 Staging, maintenance deployments and backups** · F5 · essential · L · operations — Staging (from main) and production (from tags) with separate DB and secrets. Deployment in maintenance mode: notice with a countdown, save, migrations and documented rollback. Backups: daily pg_dump and WAL for point-in-time recovery, stored off-site in S3-compatible storage with retention of 7 daily and 4 weekly. Finished when the automatic weekly restore in a disposable container passes. Pending from the user: the SMTP provider and the mail DNS (SPF, DKIM and DMARC), without which staging does not start (platform.md §4.3). Done (phase 5, P1): sending by SMTP (`ELDMARK_MAIL=smtp`, STARTTLS or `smtps://`, in the background) and the sender `ELDMARK_MAIL_FROM` (platform.md §19.2). Done (phase 5, P5): the zone handles the heartbeat's `maintenance`. It warns by NOTICE at 10, 5 and 1 min, at 30 s and at 10 s (or with what remains, if it arrives late), warns if it is cancelled and, at its time, does the orderly shutdown. That shutdown saves everyone and waits for the platform to answer before deregistering (platform.md §19.9). Done (phase 5, P9b): `platform/deploy/` with the `Dockerfile` (`platform` and `ops` images), the `docker-compose.yml` for staging and production (database, platform, Caddy, zones and the backup and check services; Docker secrets and journald in all), `staging.env.example` and `production.env.example`; `backup.sh` (`pg_dump -Fc` with 7 daily and 4 weekly and `purges.txt` next to each backup, with the most recent always at hand), `restore_check.sh` (test restore in an ephemeral cluster with `reapply-purges`, migrations up to date and integrity queries, also in the disposable container `docker compose run --rm restore-check`) and `pitr_check.sh` (point-in-time recovery with `archive_command`, `pg_basebackup` and `recovery_target_time`, in 3 s), tested against ephemeral PostgreSQLs (platform.md §19.13). Missing on the VPS (user): building and publishing the images, the compose started, WAL-G and S3, the cron with its alert and seeing the weekly restore pass; and `admin maintenance` with `deploy.sh`. Depends on: TEC-20, TEC-22.
- [x] **TEC-41 Account lifecycle** · F5 · essential · M · server, operations — Email verification, password recovery and change with single-use links that expire, email change and account deletion with a grace period, all with an attempt limit. Finished with tests of expired or reused links and of a deletion that is cancelled within the deadline. Depends on: TEC-23. Done (phase 5, P0): the `account_tokens` link table and the deletion state of `accounts` (0001_accounts); the lifecycle is P1's. Done (phase 5, P1): email verification, password recovery and change, email change with a notice to the old one and a 7-day rollback (`revert_email`), deletion with a 14-day grace period that is cancelled in time and hourly purge, single-use links (24 h, 1 h and 7 days) that are invalidated in the same transaction as the change, `/verify`, `/reset` and `/revert` pages, and limits of emails per recipient and IP; tests of expired and used links and of a deletion cancelled in time (platform.md §19.2). Mail goes out through `ELDMARK_MAIL=log` or through SMTP; the provider is the user's (TEC-28). Done (phase 5, implementation review): spending a `revert_email` voids those issued afterwards (the owner's, the oldest, leaves the thief's dead in an A→B→C chain), its email stays reserved while it lives, and `password-reset` answers without waiting for the database, so timing does not give away accounts (platform.md §19.7).
- [x] **TEC-43 Privacy, GDPR and legal documents** · F5 · essential · M · server, operations, content — Versioned privacy policy, terms and rules with acceptance recorded when creating the account, minimum age and separate consents for crash reports and analytics. Includes data export and deletion and a retention table (chat, logs, audit). Finished when a test account exports its data, is deleted and the expired records disappear according to the table. Depends on: TEC-23. Done (phase 5, P0): `legal_documents`, `legal_acceptances`, `consents`, `purge_log` and `sanctioned_email_hashes` (0001 and 0006); acceptance, export, deletion and retention, in P1 and P9a. Done (phase 5, P1): drafts of terms, privacy (with the retention table) and rules in `platform/legal/`, published in `legal_documents` on startup and accepted on registering (also new versions, with `terms_required`), age checkbox, separate and changeable consents, export of the account's data (without the characters' progress, P9a), deletion with a 14-day grace period and account purge with its fingerprint in `sanctioned_email_hashes`, and purge of unverified accounts at 7 days; all with the privacy session if the account is sanctioned (platform.md §19.2). The hourly retentions, the progress in the export, the characters in the purge and the legal review are missing (P9a and user). Done (phase 5, P2): the character purge of platform.md §11.2 (nameless tombstone, `purge_log`, `ProgressStore.Purge` and nothing with a live lease), that of those deleted on expiry of their deadline, every hour, and that of all those of an account being purged, which no longer goes unpurged because it has characters (platform.md §19.3); `reapply-purges`, the ledger retention and the progress in the export remain (P9a). Done (phase 5, P7a): `admin --actor <email> purge-now <email>` (express deletion: revokes the sessions, waits up to 30 s for its characters to leave or answers `character_busy` without purging anything) and the 30-day chat retention (platform.md §19.5). Done (phase 5, P9b): `admin --actor <email> reapply-purges <purges.txt>` (brought from P9a: deletes the backup's leases and tickets and purges accounts and characters again with their original `purged_at`, idempotent, and exits with an error if anything in `purges.txt` is left unpurged), `backup.sh` with `purges.txt` and `restore_check.sh`, which fails if the restored backup keeps personal data of what was purged or the email of a purged account in `audit_log`; journald at 30 days and Caddy without an access log (platform.md §19.13). Done (phase 5, progression.md P7): `ProgressStore.Purge` truly deletes the progress state (progress, balances, objects and timers; the ledger keeps the tombstone), `PurgeRetention` empties the ledger a year after the purge and returns the empty tombstones, and `Export` gives the record and what is the ledger's own without staff data; `purge-now` and `reapply-purges` now purge in `staging` and `production`, and `restore_check.sh` checks that what was purged keeps no progress (progression.md §19.17). The hourly ledger retention job and the progress in the export are missing (P9a). Done (phase 5, P9a): the hourly `privacy_retention` job (`internal/privacy`) applies the retention table of platform.md §11.1 (logins at 30 days; expired sessions, links and test emails at 7; tickets at 1; ended instances at 30; audit and closed reports, with their evidence, at a year; an open report closes itself at a year; the sanctions of a purged account, a year after the purge; sanctioned email fingerprints, on expiry; reserved names, when the reservation ends) and the ledger retention (`PurgeRetention` while it deletes something and the deletion of the tombstones it leaves empty); the export carries each character's progress (`ProgressStore.Export`, also the ledger that remains from the tombstones, without actor, note or detail of the staff's operations); `privacy-1.md` reviewed. Tests of the criterion: an account (banned, with the privacy session) exports its data with the progress, requests deletion and is purged, and its email does not return until its fingerprint expires; each row of the table deletes what has expired and nothing else; and a restore at the boundary against the hourly purge leaves the character whole or purged (platform.md §19.15). Pending from the user: the legal review of the drafts, of the minimum age and of the deadlines marked in platform.md §11.1, before any public test.
- [ ] **TEC-29 World service: directory, presence and messaging** · F6 · essential · L · server — Zone processes register (zone, channel, host, port, build, occupancy) and send a heartbeat every 5 s; after 15 s without a heartbeat they are marked down. The service assigns a process on each entry and handoff, stores presence and offers a bus between processes: realm, guild, group and whisper chat, and global alerts. Finished when the registration, failure and between-two-zones message tests pass. Depends on: TEC-22, TEC-24.
- [ ] **TEC-30 Zone handoff and reconnection** · F6 · essential · L · server, client, shared — HANDOFF (0x31): the origin checks that the player is at the pass and out of combat, saves, releases the lease and requests the ticket. The client loads the zone in a thread and repeats HELLO → WELCOME → CLIENT_READY; if it fails, it returns to the safe point. After a drop it reconnects with the token without going through the login. Finished when a bot goes capital → PvE → border in under 5 s per handoff, without losing or duplicating state. Includes the ZonePortal node and spawning in the last saved zone and last saved point. Depends on: TEC-25, TEC-29, UI-06.
- [ ] **TEC-31 Operations dashboards and alerts** · F6 · important · M · operations — Prometheus + Grafana, or a lightweight alternative, read /metrics from each zone and from the platform: players per zone and realm, tick p99, KB/s, logins, errors and DB latency. Centralized logs with search. Finished when alerts arrive at Discord or email for a process down, p99 above 8 ms for 5 min, disk at 80 % or a failed backup. Depends on: TEC-18, TEC-29.
- [ ] **TEC-44 Product analytics: funnel and retention** · F6 · important · M · server, operations — Anonymous events, with consent: install, account, character created, first hints, first level, first capture and session duration. A dashboard shows the first-hour funnel and D1/D7/D30 retention. Finished when a week of closed tests gives the funnel and retention without personal data. Depends on: TEC-18, TEC-22, TEC-43.
- [ ] **TEC-32 Grid-based interest for Eldrun** · F7 · essential · M · server — GridInterestPolicy: 64 m grid, entry at 200 m and exit at 230 m. The weight is clamp(1 − d/R, 0.1, 1), with an extra if there is combat with the observer, and fortresses are always relevant. Finished with hysteresis tests, the cost of the staggered recomputation with 300 entities and measurements with scattered and clustered bots. Depends on: TEC-06, TEC-15.
- [ ] **TEC-33 Delta snapshots and Eldrun budget** · F7 · essential · L · shared, server, client — Delta snapshots against ack_snapshot_tick: the server's base is exactly the one the client reconstructs. Budget of ~700 B with priority by distance, lower frequency for distant things and COMPRESS_RANGE_CODER measured. There are property tests with loss, reordering and expired bases. Target: 20 KB/s per client at most and ~6 MB/s of output with 300 players. Depends on: TEC-32.
- [ ] **TEC-34 Eldrun load test at scale** · F7 · essential · M · tools, operations — 300 combatant bots spread across several hosts against the Eldrun process on the VPS, in scattered, siege (150 within 100 m) and joins-and-leaves scenarios. Finished with a report of p99 per section, KB/s, memory and the per-battle player limit with which GUE will size the castles and Aldcor. Depends on: TEC-15, TEC-20, TEC-33, MUN-43.
- [ ] **TEC-35 Server CPU scaling** · F7 · important · L · server, shared — Levers according to what TEC-34 measures:
- build_for in parallel with WorkerThreadPool.add_group_task;
- SnapshotBuilder in C++ (GDExtension) if it goes over 3 ms with 100 bots;
- simulate() in C++ if it goes over 25 µs per character.
Each C++ version carries byte-for-byte equivalence tests with the GDScript one. Finished when TEC-34 meets a p99 below 8 ms or the real limit is documented. Depends on: TEC-34.
- [ ] **TEC-36 Eldrun capacity and queues** · F7 · important · M · server, client — Cap on Eldrun players and cap per realm. The queue lives in the world service and shows the position and estimated time on the client. The seat is reserved for 2 min after a disconnection and kicked for inactivity. Finished when the queue tests with bots entering and leaving pass. Cap per realm derived from TEC-34 and queue at the gate with position and estimated time. Depends on: TEC-29, TEC-34, GUE-36.
- [ ] **TEC-37 Cell-based visual streaming in Eldrun** · F7 · important · M · client, content — Eldrun's visual layer goes in 256 m cells: they are requested with load_threaded_request within 600 m and unloaded beyond 800 m. The castles' and Aldcor's HLODs are always loaded and the gameplay layer is loaded whole. Finished when the benchmark route does not exceed 33 ms per frame on loads and memory stays stable. Depends on: TEC-21, MUN-05, MUN-42, ART-32, ART-34.
- [ ] **TEC-38 Channels and orchestration across several hosts** · F8 · essential · L · operations, server — An agent per host starts and stops zone processes on the world service's order. Channels are opened when a zone fills up, closed after 10 min empty and the player can switch channel. Dungeon instances are created on demand and a crashed process is relocated to another host. Finished with tests of these cases and a documented capacity plan. Depends on: TEC-29, TEC-28.
- [ ] **TEC-39 Launch distribution and patches** · F8 · important · M · operations, client — According to the decision: Steam (depots, beta and staging branches, Steam ticket redeemed on the platform) or a launcher with a manifest signed in Ed25519 and patch PCKs verified with SHA-256. The minimum version is enforced at login. Finished when going from one build to the next does not force downloading the whole game. Depends on: TEC-12, TEC-23.
- [ ] **TEC-40 Hardening before launch** · F8 · essential · M · operations, server — Threat review: UDP and connection flooding, credential theft, ticket reuse and injection in names and chat. Provider anti-DDoS, DB and internal services only on a private network, nightly fuzz of the decoders in CI, reviewed limits and dependency audit. Finished when the disaster drill restores production in under 2 h. Depends on: TEC-27, TEC-38.
- [ ] **TEC-47 Remote configuration and server data patches** · F8 · important · M · server, operations — Separates the data that affects prediction (inside sim_hash) from what only the server reads (damage, mitigation, loot, prices, fortress timings). The latter are reloaded without a client patch, and per-zone feature switches are added. Finished when an attack's damage changes in staging without publishing a client and the change is audited. Depends on: CMB-02, TEC-28, TEC-29.

The hit arc does not come from the mesh but from CMB's data. Finished when the hero and the remotes wear sword and shield without clipping through the body at rest, running and jumping. The final models (Fantasy Props MegaKit and RPG Weapons Pro, human purchase), the grip point and the sheathed position are missing. Meanwhile: enable `cast_shadow` on the weapons (today disabled in `weapon_props.gd`), if the box stays, at least a diamond-shaped blade with a beveled edge, and check that the arm does not go through the shield. When swapping weapons, the accessories change mid-SWAP action and not on the first frame (`character_animator.gd` emits `weapon_shown` as soon as the id changes). Done (custom sword): the sword is no longer a box. `tools/blender/build_sword.py` models it from scratch (0.94 m blade with an edge bevel, fuller, flare and ogive tip; shaped guard; grip with the leather wraps in the geometry; about 2,900 triangles and a 750 LOD1), with code-generated textures and four GLBs in `assets/models/weapons/` (base and one variant per realm that only changes guard, pommel and colors: wolf, sun and anvil with ember). `WeaponProps` hangs `sword_base.glb` from the hand with the grip at `SWORD_GRIP_*` (the box remains as a fallback if it fails to load) and `SwordModel` distributes the LODs; `scenes/tests/sword_preview.tscn` takes the photos and `tools/render/sword_hero_shots.gd` captures the hero. Sword round 2: the contrast comes from a dark albedo with a light edge (on the game camera the first version melted into the sky and read worse than the box), heroic proportions (10.8 x 3 cm blade, without touching the tip at 0.94 m), polished edges on the metal through a three-stripe atlas, and each realm with its pommel and blade: profile wolf with a fang guard (Vardheim), half sun with an eight-ray pommel (Solmarch) and symmetric anvil with the ember crack also in the fuller (Askarra, emission 3.5). The realm's sword now comes out in the hand (`AppearanceApplier.apply_bytes` -> `HumanoidVisual.set_realm` -> `WeaponProps.set_sword_variant`), also on remotes. The shield and the sheathed position are missing. The sword's minimal trail (`SwordTrail`) is done in ART-09. Wave 1 (3 November 2026): the blade, the guard, the shield disc and the bow pieces cast shadows (`cast_shadow` in `weapon_props.gd`; the string, 6 mm, does not), and when swapping weapons the accessories change mid `SWAP` action (`CharacterAnimator.swap_show_fraction`), on the player and on remotes; `tests/test_dodge_and_swap_visual.gd`. Quality wave 4 (4 November 2026), what the critique of the custom sword left: the base blade no longer comes out almost black in the hand (its metallic drops from 0.88 to 0.45 with the medium-steel albedo slightly lighter; Vardheim's, to 0.62); Vardheim's wolf pommel stops being a disc with two eyes, which read as a cat or an owl, and is the head's own silhouette (long narrow muzzle with nose, pointed cheeks, tall separate ears, slanted eyes and brows) extruded to 3 cm of edge with a polished border; Askarra's guard goes from a wood brown to bronze (lighter and yellower, metallic 0.62 instead of 0.95, roughness 0.27); the base trail is silvery ivory and not Vardheim's blue, and the heavy-attack one is wider, opaque and saturated (see ART-09). They are regenerated with `python tools/blender/sword_textures.py build/sword/tex` and `blender --background --factory-startup --python tools/blender/build_sword.py -- --out assets/models/weapons --tex <absolute path of build/sword/tex>`; before and after in `build/o4_antes/espada/` and `build/o4_despues/espada/`.

- [x] **ART-08 Combat animation tree** · F3 · essential · L · client — Torso layer with a bone mask over the locomotion (to attack and block while walking) and full-body states (dodge, stun, death). Each clip is rescaled to the windup, active hit and recovery phases set by the shared data; the local player reads the action and its phase from PlayerState, and remotes the snapshot's action byte. - [ ] **ART-09 VFX and hit feel** · F3 · essential · L · client, content — Client-only effects, in a pool, triggered by CMB's events on their draw tick: weapon trail, impact sparks, block impact, guard break, dodge dust, death, respawn and one effect per ability of the first set. Includes local hitstop of 40-80 ms (animation only), camera shake and a flash on whoever receives the hit through material_overlay. Finished when everything uses text resources (Binbun Hit/Battle FX and Kenney Particle Pack, recorded in LICENSES.md) and 20 simultaneous impacts do not drop below 60 fps in the stress scene. Includes the rule of predicting or confirming the impact (decided only once); each ability's effect belongs to CMB-21. Quality (docs/quality.md): none of this exists (no particles, no trails, no hitstop) and `swing_active_changed`, which was documented as the trail's hook, has no listeners. Done (minimal trail, sword round 2): `SwordTrail` (`scripts/client/sword_trail.gd`) is an additive ribbon, without light or shadow and tinted per realm, between `TrailBase` and `TrailTip` with the outer edge extended to about 2.2 m from the axis (decision 8 of quality.md) and switched off in 0.13 s; `CombatAnimationLayer.is_swing_active()` launches it (the same check as `swing_active_changed` on the local one and the active phase of the action byte on remotes) and the animator notifies it with `swing_active_changed`. The rest of ART-09 is missing (impact, sparks, hitstop, block). Proposed order: (1) impact at the contact point (the victim's chest, in the attacker-victim direction) with flesh, shield and wood variants; (2) ribbon trail between guard and tip, launched with `swing_active_changed` on the local one and with the active phase of the action byte on remotes; (3) animation-only hitstop, 50 ms on lights and 80 ms on heavies, with an offset in `CombatAnimationLayer` that is recovered during the recovery, without touching the simulation. With 100 ms of RTT the confirmation already arrives in the recovery, so the hitstop and the impact sound should be predicted with a local cosmetic check: it is a pending decision that extends the "Own impact" table of combat.md. Also: blocked hit with a spark on the shield, push and stamina pulse; parry with its marker variant, white and gold flash on the weapon, 100 ms hitstop on both and a sharp sound; heavy charge with a growing glow on the blade, a pulse on reaching the maximum and a camera nudge on release (today `add_shake` is only called on taking damage); effects of the three sword-and-shield abilities legible on remotes (wall with a frontal arc with the border in the realm's color); death with 100 ms hitstop and dust on touching the ground; glow on the NPC's weapon when the windup begins. Downloads: Kenney Particle Pack and Binbun Hit FX and Battle FX Free. Depends on: ART-08, CMB-10, CMB-13. Done (quality wave 4, 4 November 2026): the effects in `scenes/vfx/` are connected to combat by `CombatVfx` (`scripts/client/vfx/combat_vfx.gd`, one node per client with a `VfxPool` per effect). (1) Impact at the contact point (the victim's chest moved forward toward the attacker, with the slash's direction) of EVERY confirmed hit, own or others', through the new signal `CombatFeedback.hit_impact` on its draw tick: flesh (`hit_flesh`), mail (`hit_metal_spark`), leather and gambeson (`hit_cloth`), block with splinters on the shield's face (`hit_block_wood`) and parry with `parry_flash` plus steel sparks, so block and parry are told apart at a glance; the heavy adds sparks. (2) Trail: `SwordTrail` is kept (which already followed `swing_active_changed` on the local one and the active phase of the action byte on remotes) with the heavy's redone (wider, opaque 0.86 against 0.5, saturated and with a drop of ember) and the base sword's in silvery ivory instead of blue; `WeaponTrail` remains for the preview. (3) Hitstop of 50 ms for lights and 80 ms for heavies, 100 ms on the parry and on death, animation only: `CombatAnimationLayer.start_hitstop` freezes the clip, accumulates an offset and recovers it at 1.5x during the recovery; it does not touch the simulation. Decision 6 applied as a cosmetic: while the own hit is active, `CombatFeedback._try_predict` checks the hit's sector (`HitShapes.sector_hits_capsule`, the same as the server's) against the DRAWN positions of hostile enemies and, if it reaches one, already launches sound, spark and hitstop; the number, the marker and the DPS keep waiting for the event, and if the server confirms it as a normal hit they are not repeated (if it turns out blocked, the block spark comes out). Remotes also do hitstop when attacking (on the HIT's draw tick). (4) Charge aura: `HumanoidVisual` hangs `charge_aura.tscn` from the sword (mounted over the real blade with `AuraMount`), grows with the state's charge fraction (local) or the seen time (remotes), pulses on reaching the maximum and bursts on release; plus a camera nudge on launching the heavy and on landing an own heavy or parry. (5) Abilities: shield strike (shield impact and dust wave), rush (two-stage dust trail) and shield wall (new arc of light, `scenes/vfx/shield_wall.tscn`, with the border in the realm's color), on the local one through `action_changed` and on remotes through the new signal `CharacterAnimator.remote_action_started`. (6) Death: body dust on touching the ground 0.42 s after the fatal hit, and dodge and hard-landing dust (with the ground's color, `VfxGround`) also on remotes. Tests in `tests/test_combat_feel.gd`. Missing: glow on the NPC's weapon when the windup begins, the block's stamina pulse, and judging the figures (50/80 ms) by playing.
- [x] **ART-10 Audio infrastructure** · F3 · essential · M · client, tools — default_bus_layout.tres with the Master (with limiter), Music, SFX, Ambience, Interface and Voices buses. A client-only AudioDirector manages pools of AudioStreamPlayer3D with a per-category voice limit, priority by distance and pitch and volume variation (AudioStreamRandomizer), and stores the volumes in user://. Finished when the architecture test confirms that the headless server does not load it and a test scene plays 64 sounds without exceeding the voice limit (yielding the farthest ones) and with the limiter on Master.
- [ ] **ART-11 Character sounds: footsteps and combat** · F3 · essential · L · client, content — Footsteps at phases 0 and 0.5 of the normalized locomotion cycle, according to the surface given by the Terrain3D texture or the collider's surface metadata (grass, dirt, stone, wood, ford water). Also jump, landing, light and heavy swishes, impacts by material (flesh, shield, wood), block, guard break, dodge, effort, pain and death, chosen from Kenney, Still North and rubberduck and converted to OGG. Finished when in the Vado Gris demo each surface sounds different and each combat event has a positional sound. Quality (docs/quality.md): the game has not a single sound: `assets/audio/` is empty, `CombatFeedback.sound_requested` has no listeners and `AudioDirector.play_3d` is not called from anywhere. Moreover, `CombatFeedback` only requests sound if the local player is attacker or victim and only observes the local character's actions, so other people's hits, swishes, dodges and deaths would not sound. First a combat audio node connected to `sound_requested` with banks per material of 3 to 5 variants and high priority for the player's own, and hooks for third parties (all HITs on their draw tick and the detection of remotes' actions); then footsteps, effort and pain. It is checked with the bench video with sound from TEC-50. Downloads: Kenney RPG Audio and Impact Sounds, Still North and 80 CC0 RPG SFX. Depends on: ART-10, ART-08. Done (quality wave 4, 4 November 2026): `CombatAudio` (`scripts/client/audio/combat_audio.gd`) is the combat audio node, created by the HUD and connected to `CombatFeedback`, with the banks of `CombatSoundBank` (`combat_sound_bank.gd`: 18 groups of 2 to 8 variants with the volume trim from `niveles.json`, random pitch and volume through `AudioStreamRandomizer`, a test checks that the table matches the JSON). The impact of EVERY confirmed hit sounds (`hit_impact`, own or others', on its draw tick, with a stable material per victim: flesh, leather, gambeson or mail, wooden or metal shield on blocking, steel clash on the parry and the Hollow's grunt) with high priority (+60 m) for the player's own; the locally predicted hit sounds without waiting for the server (decision 6) and its confirmation does not repeat it; the light and heavy swishes are requested on starting the action with the delay from `niveles.json` (`CombatAudio.swing_delay`: the sound's peak falls in the middle of the active window), also those of remotes (`CharacterAnimator.remote_action_started` with the windup of their attack); dodge, body fall 0.4 s after death, jump and landing. Footsteps: they come from the feet (`HumanoidVisual._check_feet` follows the height of the `LeftFoot` and `RightFoot` bones and the lift-to-plant edge sounds, so it matches what is seen, also on remotes and attenuated by distance) and the surface is given by `FootstepSurface` (the collider's `surface` metadata or the name of Terrain3D's painted texture: grass, dirt, stone, wood; water lowers the dirt's pitch). The grass and wood footsteps are from Kenney Impact Sounds (CC0, recorded in `assets/LICENSES.md`); everything else is still our own production. Listening sheet: `python tools/audio/listening_sheet.py` leaves in `build/o4_hoja_escucha/` one OGG per category with options A and B in sequence (what was synthesized in-house against Kenney, Still North and rubberduck, with the volume matched) and an `indice.md`; each category comes from what was synthesized except the grass and wood footsteps, and the final decision is by ear. Video with sound: `python tools/quality/run.py video --mp4`. Tests in `tests/test_combat_sound_bank.gd`. Missing: players' effort and pain, guard-break sounds, the ford's water with its own sound (today dirt with the pitch lowered), the block (raising the shield) and the user choosing by ear on the sheet.

- [ ] **ART-46 Attacks with weight: full body, planted feet and shield on guard** · F4 · essential · M · client, content — All attacks are `upper_body = true` and `CombatPoseModifier` only applies the Spine subtree: the legs come from the locomotion, which with the lunge (`lunge_speed` from 1.5 to 2.5 m/s) walks during the slash, and each hit ends in a little hop on one foot (`animacion/lado/com_combo_completo.png`, frames 2 and 8); in addition the left arm hangs loose and the shield comes out behind the shoulder. A lower-body weight per segment in `CombatAnimationLayer` and `CombatPoseModifier`: attacks are applied with hips and legs when no movement is requested, and the legs return to locomotion only according to the velocity the input asks for, without the lunge. A per-entry mask in `CombatActionAnim` that excludes the shield arm in sword attacks, which the ART-45 layer sets. `lunge_speed` adjusted so that the advance stays at what the clip can bear without sliding (about 0.3 to 0.5 m; it affects CMB-29's balance). When an attack starts, the model is placed instantly at the new yaw so that the slash comes out toward the aim point. Finished when in `com_ligero_*`, `com_combo_completo` and `com_fuerte__golpe` without movement input the feet stay planted in the windup and the hit, the hips turn toward the hit and the shield does not leave the front of the chest. Depends on: ART-07, ART-08, ART-45, CMB-06. Done (4 November 2026, wave 2): `CombatActionAnim` gains `lower_weight` (per phase), `keep_left_arm`; the lights, the heavy, the charge, the finisher and the shield push take hips and legs from the clip when the input does not ask to walk (the lunge is subtracted from the speed), the shield arm is set by the combat stance and the model is placed instantly facing the camera. The third light's windup uses the stance's legs because that clip starts with a jump. - [ ] **ART-61 Realm walls adapted to the terrain** · F4 · important · L · content, tools — Each wall is 360 m of the same 6 m piece in a straight line, without plinth or buttresses, with a gate two character heights tall under 18 m towers, scaffolds as floating blocks without supports and a portcullis that is a flat decal recolored to gold in Solmarch (`mundo/camaras_revision/muralla_vardheim.png`, `jugador_solmarch/04_puerta_de_muralla.png`). A wall that follows the terrain, stepped, with kinks in plan, battered plinth and buttresses; monumental gate with a 6 to 8 m arch, projecting towers, machicolation and a dark-iron portcullis with volume; scaffolds with corbels and a little roof; the ends broken against the mountain. The collisions can remain boxes. What is immediate (corbels and portcullis color) needs nothing; the gate and the new pieces, own modeling with the kit's textures (ART-22 and ART-36) or the Village Source. Finished when in the review cameras no wall is a straight ruler, the scaffolds have support and the gate reads as the entrance of a realm. Depends on: ART-13, MUN-04.
- [ ] **ART-62 Realm identity in each half of Vado Gris** · F4 · important · L · content — The two halves are the same valley mirrored with another flag: the same tinted brick, the same grass, the same camp (`mundo/jugador_vardheim/01_campamento_spawn.png` versus `jugador_solmarch/01_campamento_spawn.png`). If the user approves it (docs/zones/vado_gris.md today only admits models and materials), dress each half differently without touching the gameplay layer, which stays mirrored: Vardheim with frost or snow on the terrain and the rocks next to the wall, dense conifers, rough rubble masonry (another stone texture) and hide tents; Solmarch with greener grass, cypresses or poplars, limestone with ivy, vineyard terraces outside the playable area and white-and-gold pavilions; the sky and fog palette also in favor of each side. The longhouse, the round towers with a cone and the carved gate arrive with the ART-22 and ART-36 kits. Finished when in 5 random crops of each camp, without HUD, whoever has read world.md guesses the realm by shape and material, not just by the flag's color. Depends on: ART-03, ART-13, ART-59. **Done (first pass, wave 5, 4 November 2026; decision 5 of quality.md, provisional):** Vardheim to the north with patchy snow, dark forest floor under the conifers, snow on the peaks, frosted grass and the sky's horizon colder; Solmarch to the south with lively green grass and the horizon warmer. Only paint, grass and sky: the gameplay layer stays mirrored (same routes, same figures). **Missing:** rough rubble masonry versus limestone with ivy, cypresses, vineyard terraces outside the playable area and the longhouse and round towers of ART-22 and ART-36; the 5-crops-per-realm test has not been done.
- [ ] **ART-63 Ruins of Eld and recognizable chapel** · F4 · nice-to-have · M · content — The ruins are thin, pale walls like cardboard (the ash layer of `eldrun/ruin_stone.tres` whitens the upward faces) and the chapel is a 12 × 10 m low wall without gable or apse that is not recognized (`mundo/camaras_revision/ruinas_aldea.png`, `jugador_vardheim/31_capilla.png`). Darker, damper stone with moss; piles of rubble, burnt beams and ivy; thick caps on the broken walls; a chapel with gable, bell-gable and ruined apse as a vertical landmark of the peninsula; and some emissive ember crack that introduces the Ember mechanic from world.md. Finished when in the review cameras the chapel is recognized at 50 m and the ruins do not look like sheets. Depends on: ART-13, ART-54.
- [ ] **ART-64 The shield looks like a flat disc in character creation** · F4 · important · S · content, client — From the latest visual review: on the creation stage the shield is seen from the front as a flat disc, with no rim, boss or thickness. Show it with its real model (thickness, rim and boss) and a pose or turn that shows its volume. Finished when `creacion_*` shows the shield with volume in all the poses. Depends on: ART-20.
- [x] **ART-19 Character appearance system** · F5 · essential · M · shared, client, server — CharacterAppearance resource in scripts/shared with compact indices (body, head, hairstyle, beard, skin, hair and eye palettes, and visible equipment slots) that is encoded in a few bytes for the SPAWN and for the database; the server validates it and HumanoidVisual applies it. Finished when a test encodes, decodes and applies 100 random appearances without errors and the server rejects out-of-range indices. Decided in progression.md §7.2 and §7.3: 8 B without equipment slots; weapons go in the public state and visible armors will carry their own visual id with PRG-05 and PRG-06. Depends on: ART-01, TEC-04. Done (phase 5, progression.md P3): `CharacterAppearance` (8 B: `encode`, `decode`, `validate`, `random`, `default_for`), `AppearanceCatalog` with the pieces in the repo and their frozen order (`appearance_ids.txt`), the rule in Go (`progress.ValidAppearance`) and `appearance_cases.json`, which both tests read; `test_character_appearance` validates 100 random ones per race and rejects each out-of-range index (progression.md §19.4). Applying them to `HumanoidVisual` (`AppearanceApplier`, P6) and the SPAWN (P4) are missing. Done (phase 5, progression.md P4): the appearance's 8 B in the PLAYER ENTITY_SPAWN (protocol 7), not validated in `decode` (progression.md §19.9); the zone puts them with P5b and the client applies them with P6. Done (phase 5, progression.md P6): `AppearanceApplier` (pieces with `HumanoidVisual.set_part`/`clear_part`, which do not recreate the character, and skin, hair and eye tints per material) and `resolve` (an appearance that is not valid for its realm falls back to the default one), applied to the own character, to each remote and to the character window's figure; test_appearance_visual applies all piece combinations and 100 random ones per people without engine errors (progression.md §19.19). Done (phase 5, integration): the two halves of the criterion on the same branch: `test_appearance_visual` (P6) applies all piece combinations and 100 random ones per people without errors, and the server lets no out-of-range index through: the platform rejects them on creation (`ValidateCreate`, P7, with the cases of `appearance_cases.json`) and the zone swaps for the realm's default the one that is not valid on loading (P5a, `test_progress_service`). "Finished when" is met; the visual approval remains pending from the user (progression.md §15).
