## Pending decisions

- **What does Esc do now that there will be windows and a game menu? Today it toggles the free cursor (camera_rig.gd).** (decide before phase 2): Esc closes the top window and, without windows, opens the game menu; the cursor is released by holding Alt or on opening any window; Esc keeps toggling the cursor and the game menu moves to F10; Esc closes windows; without windows, the first Esc releases the cursor and the second opens the menu. Recommendation: The first: it is the universal convention, and since opening any window already releases the cursor, the toggling with Esc is almost never needed.
- **Visual style of the interface** (decide before phase 2): Dark, translucent minimalist with Eld metal and stone trim and accents in the realm's color (New World line); Ornate parchment and wood (classic MMO); Modern flat without a theme. Recommendation: Minimalist with Eld trim: it reads well in action combat, comes cheap with StyleBoxFlat and 9-patch and leaves the strong color for the sides, as world.md asks.
- **Typography (assets/fonts/ is empty)** (decide before phase 2): Serif for titles (Cinzel or Alegreya SC) plus a legible sans for the rest (Noto Sans or Inter), all OFL; A single serif for everything (EB Garamond or Alegreya); System fonts. Recommendation: Serif only in titles and Noto Sans in the body: legible at small sizes, covers Spanish and English with room to spare and the OFL license is recorded without trouble in LICENSES.md.
- **How nameplates are drawn** (decide before phase 2): Projected 2D Control (unproject_position) in a HUD layer with a pool; Label3D or Sprite3D in billboard mode in the world; a SubViewport per nameplate. Recommendation: Projected 2D Control with a pool: sharp text at any distance, uses the same Theme and is easy to limit and prioritize in Eldrun; occlusion is solved with spaced rays.
- **Where does continuous integration run and how is Git LFS handled in it?** (answered; Git LFS was dropped later, so CI has no LFS cache now) (decide before phase 2): GitHub Actions on Linux with an LFS object cache; Own runner on the development PC (Windows); No CI: a pre-push hook that runs the tests. Recommendation: Actions on Linux with the LFS cache indexed by the hash of the pointers; this way the LFS quota is not spent on each run and, in addition, the server's platform is tested. Move to an own runner only if the cache is not enough.
- **How are the animations that the free packs lack obtained (8-direction strafe, block impact, directional dodge, bow)?** (decide before phase 2): UAL1 Pro ($9.99), without confirming that it includes strafe; UAL1 Source and UAL2 Source (about $30 in total); KayKit Character Animations retarget; Own animation in Blender on the UAL rig; Mixamo (not CC0). Recommendation: Buy UAL1 Source, which does not depend on the doubt about Pro, and retarget from KayKit only what is missing; Mixamo is discarded because of its license. Updated with the research in [quality.md](quality.md) (1 November 2026): UAL1's official image confirms that Pro includes the 120+ animations, with 8-direction jog, left and right dodge, directional hits, second death and crawling; UAL2 Source (there is no Pro) includes the family's only 8-direction walk and the bow clips. The backward dodge and blocking while walking are still uncovered. New recommendation: UAL1 Pro plus UAL2 Source ($24.98), same skeleton and without retargeting; KayKit or an own clip only for the backward dodge.
- **How is an ally told apart from an enemy in the thick of combat without target selection?** (decide before phase 2): Only tabard and side color; Tabard plus a colored outline or rim on enemies; Tabard plus a colored nameplate (UI); All three, with the outline as an accessibility option. Recommendation: Tabard with color and emblem plus nameplate, and the enemy outline as an accessibility option: it does not depend on color alone and does not clutter the scene by default.
- **When does controller support arrive?** (decide before phase 3): From launch; After launch, with the interface navigable by focus from the start; Never. Recommendation: After launch, but with focus neighbors in the UI-09 components from phase 3, so as not to redo all the windows.
- **Where is the client distributed to testers and, later, to the public?** (decide before phase 3): itch.io with butler for the tests and Steam at launch; Steam (Playtest) from the closed tests; Own launcher with a signed manifest and patch PCK; Manual zip. Recommendation: itch.io with butler now, because it gives restricted access and free delta patches, and Steam at launch. An own launcher costs several sessions and adds attack surface without real advantage.
- **On what platform is the dedicated server exported?** (decide before phase 3): Linux x86_64; Windows Server; Linux arm64. Recommendation: Linux x86_64: images and VPSs are light and cheap, and reconciliation absorbs the last-bit differences with Windows clients. arm64 would add another libm and Jolt combination that would have to be measured separately.
- **What happens to the capture progress if the circle is left empty?** (decide before phase 4): It is kept as is; It decays gradually after a few seconds empty; It goes back to zero. Recommendation: Let it decay at a third of the capture speed after 5 s empty: it rewards keeping up the pressure without erasing all the effort over a single death.
- **Right after capturing, while there is no captain, can the fort be captured again?** (decide before phase 4): Yes, at once, because there is no captain; A 2 min lockout (circle inactive, gate damageable) until the garrison arrives; Immediate captain and guards at 2 min. Recommendation: A 2 min lockout until the garrison arrives, which fits the timer of world.md: it avoids capture ping-pong and gives the winner time to reorganize.
- **Minimap and zone map technique** (decide before phase 4): Top-down image baked by a tool from the zone, with icons on top; SubViewport with a live top-down camera; Hand-illustrated map per zone. Recommendation: Baked by a tool: it costs nothing at runtime, is deterministic like the Vado Gris generator and is stylized with a shader; drawing them by hand does not scale to more than 15 zones.
- **Do enemies appear on the minimap?** (decide before phase 4): Never; Always, within a radius; Only revealed: Brenn Watchtower, ally alerts or in combat with you. Recommendation: Only revealed: it gives value to world.md's Brenn Watchtower and to communication, without turning the minimap into a radar that cancels the reading of the battlefield.
- **Which provider hosts the test and production servers?** (decide before phase 4): European VPS or dedicated with UDP anti-DDoS (OVHcloud, Hetzner); Big cloud (AWS, GCP, Azure); Server at home. Recommendation: OVHcloud or Hetzner. They include a lot of outbound traffic and UDP protection, and a full zone puts out about 2.4 MB/s (close to 6 TB per month), which on a big cloud is billed dearly.
- **Where does the game's music come from?** (decide before phase 4): Existing CC0 or CC-BY music (OpenGameArt, Free Music Archive), recorded in LICENSES.md; A stock library with a commercial license for games; Commission to a composer; AI-generated music. Recommendation: Provisional CC0 music in phase 4 to validate the adaptive system, and a commission or a library with a commercial license before launch. Not AI: its legal situation is uncertain and it breaks LICENSES.md's traceability.
- **What model is used for the Hollow (the fort's neutral defenders)?** (decide before phase 4): Quaternius's Bestiary (QAL license), if it includes a skeleton; Plan B: base body with Peasant outfit, bone material and emissive eyes; Own skeleton modeled on the UAL rig. Recommendation: Plan B now, because it is CC0, uses the same rig and does not block phase 4. The Bestiary only if someone confirms it includes a skeleton and the repo is still private. Updated ([quality.md](quality.md)): while it is decided, ART-52 gives a quick visual profile (shieldless Hollow, gray bone and emissive eyes; bigger Captain) and ART-47 their clips; downloading the Bestiary's Standard (free) is enough to know whether it includes a skeleton.
- **What AI architecture do NPCs use?** (decide before phase 4): Own hierarchical state machine in GDScript with parameters in NpcDefinition; Behavior trees with LimboAI (GDExtension); Utility AI (action scoring). Recommendation: Own state machine: it is enough for guards, packs and bosses with patterns, is deterministic and easy to test in headless, and does not add another GDExtension with LFS binaries to validate on the server; inside the combat state one can score which attack to choose.
- **How is the spawn camp protected from whoever camps in it?** (decide before phase 4): Safe zone volume without damage for the owning realm; Very strong NPC guardians that attack intruders (DAoC style); Both; Nothing: the map layout is enough. Recommendation: Both: the volume guarantees that respawning is never dying again and the guardians make that rule visible in the world.
- **What match structure does Vado Gris have in the tests?** (decide before phase 4): The fort returns to neutral every N minutes with a summary at the end; No reset: the owner keeps it until someone else takes it. Recommendation: Reset to neutral every 30 min with a summary (GUE-17) while it is a test map; in Eldrun the owner keeps the fortress, as vision.md says.
- **In which region are the servers hosted?** (decide before phase 4): Europe; Eastern US (closer to Latin America); Both, with a world per region. Recommendation: Europe for the tests; before opening to the public, measure the latency of the Latin American public (180-300 ms to Europe), which the 24-tick (~250 ms) rewind cap does not fully cover.
- **What style does the baked zone map have (UI-51)?** (decide before closing phase 4; blocks wave 3 of [quality.md](quality.md)): Dark parchment (grainy paper, mountains in ink, river with a bluish-gray wash, roads with a double stroke, tree stamps); Dark tactical map (color planes, contour lines, clean icons); Return to a stylized top-down render of the terrain itself. Recommendation: Dark parchment in the map window, for tone, and the same layers with more contrast on the minimap, where legibility rules; everything comes from the same masks, so changing style later is cheap.
- **What time of day and weather does Vado Gris have (ART-54)?** (decide before closing phase 4; blocks wave 5 of [quality.md](quality.md)): Overcast sky as now, with a real directional light; A storm that clears: broken clouds and a low sun from the west at 25-30° that slips between them; Eldrun's dark storm. Recommendation: The storm that clears: it keeps the gray of Eldrun's frontier that world.md asks for but gives shadows and volume, which do not exist with a closed sky; the sun stays on the west axis and the symmetry does not change. It qualifies the pending recommendation on the day and night cycle ("fixed storm light in Vado Gris").
- **Can the two halves of Vado Gris look different (ART-62)?** (decide before closing phase 4): No, only models and materials, as docs/zones/vado_gris.md says; Yes, different terrain, vegetation, sky and architecture as long as the gameplay layer (playable heights, collisions, markers) stays mirrored. Recommendation: Yes: today the two camps are the same photo with another flag, and each realm's identity is half the charm of the war between realms; fairness is guaranteed by the gameplay layer, not by the look.
- **Are hitstop and impact sound predicted locally (ART-09, ART-11)?** (decide before closing phase 4): No, as the "Own impact" table of combat.md says today (only the sweep, trail and weapon sound are predicted); Yes, as a cosmetic, with a local check against the drawn positions, while the number and the marker keep waiting for the server. Recommendation: Yes: with 100 ms of RTT the confirmation already arrives in the recovery, outside the 100 ms active window, and an ill-timed hitstop is useless; if the server does not confirm it, all that was seen is a hit that did not subtract health.
- **Are combat ticks changed if the clips do not match (ART-07, CMB-29)?** (decide before closing phase 4): No, clips are always adapted to the ticks; Yes, only where no clip segment fits between 0.7x and 1.5x, with a new balance pass. Recommendation: Trim clips first and touch ticks only where there is no other way out, starting with the heavy's windup and recovery.
- **How is the hit's reach matched with the drawn blade (CMB-29)?** (decide before closing phase 4): A 1.1 m sword and a trail that draws up to 2.2 m, without touching the 2.6 m reach; Lower the reach to 2.2 m; More advance toward the target during the windup. Recommendation: The first: with latency a generous reach forgives, and what fails is that it cannot be seen.
- **Which backward dodge is used (ART-49)?** (decide before closing phase 4): A roll oriented where one travels (the body turns its back); A short step or hop backward, with an own clip or KayKit's; The `dodge_back` of Hadukis's Medieval Animation Pack ($19.99) after testing it. Recommendation: The short step backward: it is the most used dodge in combat, does not force turning one's back on the enemy and no pack of the family includes a backward roll.
- **What frames does the interface carry (UI-02, ART-12)?** (decide before closing phase 4): Own iron 9-slice frames made from SVG in `tools/ui`, on the dark minimalist style; kibbitz's Fantasy RPG UI Kit ($0.24, labeled "AI Assisted" in the graphics); Keep flat rectangles. Recommendation: Own frames: no cost, no doubts about provenance and applied to all windows at once from `UIThemeBuilder`.
- **What penalty does dying have?** (decide before phase 6): Only durability loss and the respawn time; XP loss (DAoC); Dropping part of the inventory (free loot). Recommendation: Only durability: in massive realm battles dying is constant, free loot would punish newcomers and repair is already the main gold sink.
- **Is loot personal or shared?** (decide before phase 6): Personal: each player with a contribution receives their own roll; Shared with group distribution (rolling dice, round robin); Whoever picks it up first keeps it. Recommendation: Personal: each player with a contribution receives their own roll (PRG-21); SOC-23 only distributes XP and credit.
- **What binding rules do objects have?** (decide before phase 6): Everything can be traded; Bound on equip in general and bound on pickup for war rewards and legendaries; Everything bound on pickup. Recommendation: Bound on equip in general, and on pickup for war items: trading in crafting and loot stays alive, and war equipment is not bought with gold.
- **Is the economy one per realm or common to the three?** (decide before phase 6): Separate per realm: market and trade only within the realm; Neutral global market; Neutral market with a high fee (smuggling). Recommendation: Separate per realm: the realms only touch each other in Eldrun as enemies, and this avoids espionage and gold laundering between sides.
- **In what format are design data (objects, loot, recipes, shops) created and balanced?** (decide before phase 6): .tres edited by hand in the editor; CSV tables in the repo that a tools/ generator turns into .tres; External design database. Recommendation: CSV with a deterministic generator, like the Vado Gris terrain: balancing hundreds of objects in a spreadsheet is much faster, and the generated .tres files are still reviewable text.
- **How do level and equipment influence Eldrun?** (decide before phase 6): Eldrun only for max-level characters; Access from a minimum level with scaling to the cap and a gear score limit; Fully normalized stats in PvP, with no influence from equipment; Entry only at max level (40); Minimum level 30 and base stats normalized to 40 (equipment still adds); Free entry without normalizing. Recommendation: Minimum level 30 with base stats normalized to 40 and a bounded equipment advantage (score cap, at most 15 %): skill weighs more than level and progression still counts. The test Vado Gris has no levels.
- **Mutual or one-way friendship?** (decide before phase 6): Mutual, with a request that must be accepted; One-way, like a follow list. Recommendation: Mutual with a request, so that nobody can watch when another player connects without their permission.
- **Range of the trade channel?** (decide before phase 6): The whole realm; Only in capitals and cities; No channel; auction house only. Recommendation: Only in capitals and cities, so that it does not flood the chat on the battlefield; reviewed if PRG adds an auction house.
- **Alliances between guilds?** (decide before phase 6): No alliances; Alliances with a shared chat (DAoC style). Recommendation: No alliances at launch: open bands and the war channel already coordinate the realm, and alliances tend to form a bloc that hoards the realm's decisions.
- **How are zone processes orchestrated?** (decide before phase 6): Static docker compose, one container per zone; Own agent per host controlled by the world service; Kubernetes + Agones. Recommendation: Static compose until phase 6, when zones are few and fixed, and an own agent in 8 for channels and instances. Kubernetes with Agones is too much operations for one person.
- **Which realm is built first in phase 6?** (decide before phase 6): Vardheim (Isenholm, Hondal, Wolf March): there are already pines, palisade and snow in the shader; Solmarch (Valcorin, Amber Fields): the Village MegaKit covers almost all its architecture. Recommendation: Vardheim, to reuse what already exists from Vado Gris (pines, palisade, dark wood, snow in the shader); MUN preferred Solmarch because of the Village MegaKit. Confirmed after validating a modeled piece (Blender risk).
- **Is there a day and night cycle?** (decide before phase 6): Full cycle in all zones; Cycle in the realms; fixed light in Eldrun and in the border test; Fixed light in all zones; The same cycle across the whole world; Fixed storm light in Eldrun and Vado Gris; cycle only in the realms; Cycle in Eldrun with a guaranteed minimum brightness. Recommendation: Cycle only in the realms; fixed storm light in Eldrun and in Vado Gris, as world.md says: a dark night in PvP is unfair and worsens legibility.
- **Which weapons will have a full animation set at launch?** (decide before phase 6): Only sword and shield; Sword and shield, bow, two-handed weapon and staff; A list tied to the classes, when they are defined. Recommendation: Four weapons (sword and shield, bow, two-handed and staff): they cover melee, ranged and support with what UAL2's Source and KayKit offer, without animating from scratch.
- **Where do the 2D images (loading screens, title background, promotional art) come from?** (decide before phase 6): In-engine captures with painterly post-processing; Commission to an illustrator; AI generation. Recommendation: In-engine captures with post-processing: free, always faithful to the real game and with no license doubts. Commission an illustrator only for the main launch image.
- **How many PvE zones does each realm have at launch?** (decide before phase 6): The four bands of world.md (1-10, 10-20, 20-30 and border 30-40); Capital, 1-10, 10-20 and border; the 20-30 after launch, with the level bands adjusted; Capital, one PvE zone and border. Recommendation: Capital, two PvE zones and border per realm: that is 12 zones instead of 15, each can be populated well, and the heart of the game is Eldrun and not PvE; PRG would have to adjust the level bands.
- **In what format are quests and dialogues written?** (decide before phase 6): Typed .tres resources written by hand or by agents, with a reference validator; Own text format (JSON) in data/ converted to .tres by a tool; Dialogue addon (Dialogic). Recommendation: Typed .tres with a validator: they are text (they comply with CLAUDE.md), the server loads them without depending on the UI and there is no conversion step; Dialogic is designed for the client and its logic is not validated on the server.
- **What size are the maps? (vision.md: "Map sizes", to be defined)** (decide before phase 6): The figures of world.md (PvE zones of 1.5 × 1.5 km, Eldrun of 3 × 3 km); Smaller zones until performance is measured. Recommendation: The figures of world.md as a target, confirmed after measuring TEC-21 and TEC-34; MUN-42 sets Eldrun at 3 × 3 km.
- **Who decides what the realm's Embers are spent on?** (decide before phase 7): Automatic: on reaching a threshold the next blessing of a rotation is activated; Vote of the players weighted by realm rank; Council formed by the leaders of the guilds with the most merits; Automatic: blessings are activated on reaching thresholds; Vote of players with rank; Council of officers or guilds; Mix: the siege is bought by anyone with minimum rank and blessings are automatic. Recommendation: Mix: blessings activate by themselves on reaching thresholds and the siege is bought by any player with war rank 2 or more, with an hourly cap. No politics or treasury sabotage until there are guilds.
- **Can fortresses be attacked at any hour?** (decide before phase 7): 24/7 unchanged; Fixed time windows per fortress; 24/7 with reinforced NPC defenders when few of the owning realm's defenders are connected. Recommendation: 24/7 with NPCs reinforced according to the defenders connected: it keeps DAoC's living world without night captures coming out free.
- **How is Aldcor captured and what bonus do the other two realms receive (world.md leaves it to be defined)?** (decide before phase 7): Like a big castle: three rings and a final circle; Hold three Ember altars at once; Hold the Flame chamber X min against the other two realms. Recommendation: Three rings with gates that only fall to siege and, at the end, hold the Flame chamber for 120 s; for the rivals, +20 % renown and siege damage against the owner. It reuses GUE-23/25 and gives a three-sided climax.
- **Is the player cap in Eldrun global or per realm?** (decide before phase 7): Global cap by order of arrival; Per-realm cap of 1/3 of the total; No cap, degrading the tick frequency. Recommendation: Per-realm cap of 1/3 of the total measured in TEC-34, with a queue at the gate: no realm wins by numbers and the tick is protected.
- **What does the outnumbered realm receive?** (decide before phase 7): Only rewards (more renown); Combat stats (defense or damage); NPC reinforcements in its fortresses. Recommendation: More renown and reinforced NPC guards, never player stats: in action combat, altering damage is perceived as unfair.
- **Can defenders repair gates and walls?** (decide before phase 7): No; only automatic regeneration; Yes, spending materials from Odd's Quarry or Embers; Yes, free, with an interruptible channel. Recommendation: In phase 4, only automatic regeneration; from phase 7, repair with materials, which gives defenders work and gives Odd's Quarry value.
- **What does claiming a fortress give a guild?** (decide before phase 7): The claim does not exist; Cosmetic claim: heraldry, name on the map and alerts; Claim with advantages: guard upgrades or part of the Embers. Recommendation: Cosmetic first: mechanical advantages concentrate power in large guilds, and are only added if tests show that involving them more in the war is needed.
- **Size of war bands?** (decide before phase 7): 4 groups of 5 (20); 6 groups of 5 (30); According to Eldrun's measured capacity. Recommendation: Set it with TEC's measurement of Eldrun's capacity starting from 4 groups (20), because large bands multiply the server cost of action combat.
- **Eldrun war map: 2D or 3D war table?** (decide before phase 7): 2D baked with the same tool as the rest of the maps; 3D war table (e.g. KayKit Medieval Hexagon, cited in art.md); 2D, with an optional 3D view in the realm panel later. Recommendation: Baked 2D: it reuses UI-20, is legible and cheap; the 3D table mixes art families, which art.md advises against, and can wait until after launch.
- **How is Eldrun's load reduced if it cannot hold 60 Hz?** (decide before phase 7): 60 Hz in all zones with grid interest and delta; Eldrun at 30 Hz announced in WELCOME.tick_rate; Simulation at 60 Hz and snapshots at 15-20 Hz for distant things; Split Eldrun into sectors (fronts and Aldcor). Recommendation: Keep the simulation at 60 Hz and lower only the frequency of distant snapshots. Changing physics_ticks_per_second on entering Eldrun breaks the determinism and the sim_hash that networking.md sets, and a server at 30 Hz with two substeps saves no simulation CPU.
- **Will there be mounts?** (decide before phase 7): No: distances designed for walking and respawn at fortresses; Only in the realms; In the realms and on Eldrun's roads, dismounting on taking damage; No mounts at launch; Only in realm zones; Everywhere, with dismounting on taking damage and forbidden near fortresses. Recommendation: No mounts at launch: Eldrun's distances are measured at a run. Reviewed after measuring phase 7 travel times, starting with the realm zones.
- **What fast travel is there?** (decide before phase 7): None: everything on foot; Network of points inside each realm (capital, villages, border garrison) with a cost, never reaching Eldrun; Free teleport to any discovered point, Eldrun included. Recommendation: Network inside the realm: it shortens the 20 min from the capital to the wall without trivializing the war, because in Eldrun mobility comes from own fortresses and the Ember lines.
- **What rewards does dominating the war zone give? (vision.md: to be defined)** (decide before phase 7): Embers for the realm treasury and blessings (GUE-22, GUE-30); Experience and loot bonuses for the whole realm; Access to exclusive content. Recommendation: Embers to the realm treasury, which feed automatic blessings and siege (GUE-22, GUE-30): a reward for the whole realm without giving excessive vertical power.
- **What shape does the moderation tool take?** (decide before phase 8): Internal web on the accounts backend; Godot application in tools/; Only in-game commands. Recommendation: Minimal internal web on the accounts backend that TEC chooses: support staff do not need the game client and can use it from any machine.
- **Are dungeons instanced or open?** (decide before phase 8): Instanced for groups of 3-5 in the PvE zones; Open dungeon in Eldrun only for the dominant realm (DAoC's Darkness Falls style); Both. Recommendation: Instanced first, which reuse passes, bosses and the instance plan of world.md; the open one under Aldcor is proposed to GUE as a dominance reward, which vision.md has to define.
- **Do seasons reset the war map?** (decide before phase 9): Persistent map without seasons; 3-month seasons that reset fortresses and ranking; Ranking-only seasons. Recommendation: Ranking-only seasons, and a map reset only if one realm dominates Aldcor for several weeks in a row: it keeps persistence without letting the map freeze.

## Decisions made

- Godot 4.7 engine with GDScript, a single project for client and headless server ([vision.md](vision.md), [../CLAUDE.md](../CLAUDE.md)).
- World "The Broken Crown": Vardheim, Solmarch and Askarra at war over Eldrun ([vision.md](vision.md), [world.md](world.md)).
- New World-style action combat: over-the-shoulder camera, crosshair, no target selection ([vision.md](vision.md)).
- Authoritative server, world divided into zones with one server process each ([vision.md](vision.md)).
- Client prediction, interpolation of remotes and hitbox rewind on the server ([vision.md](vision.md), [networking.md](networking.md)).
- ENet transport with ENetMultiplayerPeer and own binary protocol, simulation at 60 Hz ([networking.md](networking.md)).
- No collision between characters in phase 2 ([networking.md](networking.md)).
- First playable goal: Vado Gris, the frontier between Vardheim and Solmarch with a capturable fort ([vision.md](vision.md), [world.md](world.md)).
- Quaternius "Universal + MegaKit" art direction, a single kit recolored per realm; Terrain3D and ProtonScatter ([art.md](art.md)).
- Animations without root motion: movement is decided by the shared simulation ([art.md](art.md)).
- Scenes and resources in text; binaries as normal files, no Git LFS ([../CLAUDE.md](../CLAUDE.md)).
- Character names (provisional, taken at night; to be reviewed): 3-16 Latin letters with accents and ñ, no spaces or digits, initial capital and a list of reserved names; unique among those connected in the zone with the normalized key (lowercase and without accents; ñ counts as n), and global uniqueness arrives with SOC-13 (done in phase 5, P2: the unique key per world in the database, platform.md §4.4). The cap of 24 characters and 48 B of the HELLO remains as the protocol's limit. Implemented in `NameRules` (SOC-01); the client's default name goes from `Jugador-NNNN` to `Jugador` plus four letters.
- **What is seen of enemy players and what can the realms communicate?** Of the enemy only the color and emblem of their side; the name only when aiming at them with the crosshair and always in the combat log and on the death screen; no chat, whispers or groups between realms, filtered on the server (provisional, taken at night; see [social.md](social.md#0-decisions)).
- **Are gestures seen between realms?** Only the animation, without text (provisional, taken at night; see [social.md](social.md#0-decisions)).
- **Maximum group size?** 5; large battles are covered with bands of up to 4 groups (provisional, taken at night; see [social.md](social.md#0-decisions)).
- **Integrated voice chat?** No voice until after launch; the pings and marks of SOC-10 solve tactics (provisional, taken at night; see [social.md](social.md#0-decisions)).
- **Fixed classes or a classless system tied to the weapon? (vision.md: "Clases" (Classes), to be defined)** No classes: the role is given by the equipped weapons (provisional, taken at night; see [combat.md](combat.md#0-decisions)).
- **How many weapons are equipped and how does one swap between them?** Two slots with quick swap (CMB-24, phase 4); in phase 3 only sword and shield (provisional, taken at night; see [combat.md](combat.md#0-decisions)).
- **How are the numeric values of stats, damage and currencies represented?** Integers for health, stamina, damage and currencies (provisional, taken at night; see [combat.md](combat.md#0-decisions)).
- **Without target selection, where is the enemy's health seen?** On their nameplate when aiming at them or after damaging them in the last 5 s; boss bar for captains (provisional, taken at night; see [combat.md](combat.md#0-decisions)).
- **Is the impact of an own hit shown when predicted or when the server confirms it?** Mixed: sweep, trail and sound when predicted; marker, sparks and number on confirmation (provisional, taken at night; see [combat.md](combat.md#0-decisions)).
- **With the rewind, which defensive state of the target counts for the block, the parry and the dodge's invulnerability?** It protects if the defender was defending on the rewound tick or the current one, with the clarifications of combat.md §4.3 (provisional, taken at night; see [combat.md](combat.md#0-decisions)).
- **Is there friendly fire between players of the same realm?** No friendly fire (provisional, taken at night; see [combat.md](combat.md#0-decisions)).
- **How do criticals work?** Positional without chance: from behind ×1.5 (provisional, taken at night; see [combat.md](combat.md#0-decisions)).
- **In what unit are the durations of attacks, controls and cooldowns written in the .tres files?** Seconds in the data, converted to 60 Hz ticks with roundi on loading (provisional, taken at night; see [combat.md](combat.md#0-decisions)).
- **Does sprinting spend stamina?** No (provisional, taken at night; see [combat.md](combat.md#0-decisions)).
- **Does the parry exist and how is it activated?** A 6-tick window on pressing block, with a cooldown, only against melee (provisional, taken at night; see [combat.md](combat.md#0-decisions)).
- **How do humanoid NPCs (the Hollow, guards, bandits) move and simulate?** PlayerCharacter with synthetic input (provisional, taken at night; see [combat.md](combat.md#0-decisions)).
- **Is there collision between characters? (without it, Vado Gris's 4 m bridge cannot be plugged with a body)** No collision in phase 3; reviewed in phase 4 at the bridge and the gate (provisional, taken at night; see [combat.md](combat.md#0-decisions)).
- **How is respawn timed in the war zones?** A fixed 10 s with a button in phase 3; 15 s waves in Vado Gris with GUE-14 (provisional, taken at night; see [combat.md](combat.md#0-decisions)).
- **Combat HUD layout and secondary resource (vision.md only talks about stamina)** Action layout: health, stamina and action bar at the bottom center, stamina hides when full and the secondary resource slot stays reserved for the staves (phase 6) (provisional, taken at night; see [abilities.md](abilities.md#0-decisions)).
- **What resource do abilities consume?** Only cooldown time on physical weapons; mana only on the staves (phase 6), so that stamina remains the defensive resource (provisional, taken at night; see [abilities.md](abilities.md#0-decisions)).
- **How are the projectiles' origin and direction computed with the over-the-shoulder camera, if PlayerInput only carries yaw and pitch?** Canonical camera ray from yaw, pitch and a shoulder bit (`AIM_LEFT`, bit 10 of PlayerInput); the arrow leaves from the chest toward the aimed point, so it does not go through corners (provisional, taken at night; see [abilities.md](abilities.md#0-decisions)).
- **On reaching 0 health does one die outright or stay knocked down?** Knocked down in PvP (20 s) when the fatal hit is from a player of another realm; direct death against NPCs and the environment (provisional, taken at night; see [abilities.md](abilities.md#0-decisions)).
- **What technology is the platform service (accounts, sessions, persistence and directory) made with?** Own service in Go + PostgreSQL, without ORM or web framework, in `platform/` (provisional, taken at night; see [platform.md](platform.md#0-decisions)).
- **What strategy does the zone use to save characters' state?** A checkpoint every 60 s if there were changes plus immediate writing of critical events, on leaving and on closing the zone, with lease, sequence and idempotency (provisional, taken at night; see [platform.md](platform.md#0-decisions)).
- **Will there be a single world (shard) or several independent worlds?** A single world at launch, with `world_id` in the schema from the first migration (provisional, taken at night; see [platform.md](platform.md#0-decisions)).
- **How is the traffic between the client and the zone protected?** ENet's DTLS if its cost measured in TEC-27 fits, with the zone's certificate delivered by the platform over HTTPS and pinned on the client; without DTLS there are no public tests (provisional, taken at night; see [platform.md](platform.md#0-decisions)).
- **Can one change realm and how many realms can an account have?** One realm per account and world; changing the whole account with a 30-day wait comes later, and in phase 5 the realm is only released 30 days after deleting the last character (provisional, taken at night; see [platform.md](platform.md#0-decisions)).
- **Does ignoring affect one character or the whole account?** The account, silently; the server also filters the public channels (provisional, taken at night; see [platform.md](platform.md#0-decisions)).
- **Chat log retention?** 30 days for all channels, whispers included, and a year for what is copied into a report; legal review (GDPR) before the public tests (provisional, taken at night; see [platform.md](platform.md#0-decisions)).
- **By what criterion is DTLS's cost (TEC-27) accepted?** Relative: with 64 bots, DTLS does not raise the tick's p99 by more than 10 % and the absolute figure is recorded; the absolute 8 ms is not met even without DTLS (provisional, taken at night in the review; pending from the user; see [platform.md](platform.md#0-decisions)) Measured in P9c: +5.9 %, with a p99 of 28.5 ms with DTLS and 64 real clients (networking.md §14).
- **Where does a character return on entering and with how much health?** The rule of progression.md §8: in RVR zones (all those of phase 5), always the realm's camp; in the others, the saved place if it is the same zone and channel, less than 15 min ago and without combat, and if not, the camp; health, regenerated with the elapsed time (provisional, taken at night in the review; see [progression.md](progression.md#8-persistent-combat-configuration-cmb-30)).
- **Are spendable merits and realm points separate or a single figure?** Two counters fed by the same event: spendable merits and realm points, which only go down on reverting the merits that gave them (progression.md I9) (provisional, taken at night; see [fortress.md](fortress.md#0-decisions) and [progression.md](progression.md#0-decisions)).
- **What races does each realm have? (vision.md: "Razas jugables" (Playable races), to be defined)** One human people per realm, purely aesthetic (hairstyles, beards, marks and the cut of the outfits), with the same skeleton, the same capsule and no racial stats (provisional, taken at night; see [progression.md](progression.md#0-decisions)).
- **Is the inventory limited by slots or by weight?** Slots in the bag (30, expandable) and weight only on the equipment worn, which sets PRG-06's load category (provisional, taken at night; see [progression.md](progression.md#0-decisions)).
- **Are currencies and the bank per character or per account?** Per character (provisional, taken at night; see [progression.md](progression.md#0-decisions)).
- **Without target selection, who receives experience, quest progress and loot on killing an enemy?** Contribution: everyone who deals at least 10 % of the damage in the last 900 ticks receives their reward whole, with personal loot (provisional, taken at night; see [progression.md](progression.md#0-decisions)).
- **Does level change the stats in phase 5?** No: the base sheet is the same at any level until the PvE zone (PRG-22), the attributes (PRG-17) and Eldrun's normalization (PRG-31) (provisional, taken at night; see [progression.md](progression.md#0-decisions)).
- **What equipment is there in phase 5?** The two weapons and the potions; armors, the StatBlock and the derived sheet arrive with PRG-01 and PRG-06, and equipment slots 2 to 6 stay reserved (provisional, taken at night; see [progression.md](progression.md#0-decisions)).
- **Do long cooldowns run while the player is disconnected (CMB-30)?** Yes: their end is stored in UTC; in phase 5 only /atascado reaches 30 s (provisional, taken at night; see [progression.md](progression.md#8-persistent-combat-configuration-cmb-30)).
- **Is anything saved without the platform?** No: without --platform every character is ephemeral, with an in-memory inventory; local persistence uses the development platform (provisional, taken at night; see [progression.md](progression.md#94-without-platform-ephemeral-characters)).
- **Localization format and text in server messages:**
  - gettext (`.po`), with English as the source text (`msgid`) and `locale/es.po` holding the current Spanish texts;
  - context (`msgctxt`) for ambiguous words and per content type, gettext plurals and named placeholders; already formatted text is never stored;
  - the catalog lives in a private translation domain and everything goes through `L10n`, with its own pseudolocalization (Godot 4.7.2's breaks `{name}` and BBCode and does not touch plurals);
  - the server sends codes plus parameters (NOTICE, REJECT and system messages, protocol 8) and the client composes the sentences;
  - tests always run in English, with strict mode and a Spanish snapshot of every migrated screen;
  - logs, debug output and replies to staff are in English and are not translated.
  It replaces the earlier recommendation (Spanish `msgid`) because the code and the documentation are already in English and English is the fallback for any language without a translation ([localization.md](localization.md)).
- **Default language during localization:** "Automatic" is Spanish until UI-39, so that nobody sees a half-translated game without asking for it; from UI-39 on it follows the system language, with English as the fallback ([localization.md](localization.md) section 4.3).
- **Proper names in English:** descriptive ones are translated (Puente del Norte → North Bridge, los Huecos → the Hollow, Capitán del Vado → Ford Captain) and invented ones are kept (Vado Gris, Eldrun, Eld, Vardheim, Solmarch, Askarra). All of them go through the catalog ([localization.md](localization.md) section 8.2).
- **Chat command aliases:** canonical English id; the long names are valid in both languages and the short aliases follow the client's language ([localization.md](localization.md) section 9).
- **Binding legal text:** Spanish, with an English translation that says so ([localization.md](localization.md), TEC-62).
